/**
 * KOREAN CORE — single-file Cloudflare Worker (backend + frontend + auto-migrations)
 *
 * راه‌اندازی (۳ مرحله، بدون هیچ فایل دیگری):
 *  1) Cloudflare → Workers & Pages → Create → Hello World → Edit code → کل این فایل را جایگزین کن → Deploy
 *  2) همان Worker → Settings → Bindings → Add → D1 database → اسم متغیر دقیقاً:  DB
 *     (دیتابیس را اگر نداری از Storage & Databases → D1 → Create بساز)
 *  3) آدرس *.workers.dev را باز کن. جدول‌ها خودکار ساخته می‌شوند.
 *
 * با Wrangler: wrangler.jsonc حداقلی —
 *   { "name": "korean-core", "main": "worker.js", "compatibility_date": "2026-11-07",
 *     "d1_databases": [{ "binding": "DB", "database_name": "korean-core-db", "database_id": "<ID>" }] }
 *   و بعد:  npx wrangler deploy
 *
 * اگر روی همان Worker قبلی (با ASSETS) می‌ریزی، فقط کدش را عوض کن؛ بایندینگ DB سر جایش می‌ماند.
 */

const __M = {};

// ═══════════════════════════ BACKEND ═══════════════════════════
// ┌─ module: security ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.security = (() => {
const encoder = new TextEncoder();

function randomToken(bytes = 32) {
  const data = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(bytes));
  return Array.from(data, b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

async function sha256(value) {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', encoder.encode(value));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest), b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

async function hashPassword(password, salt = randomToken(16)) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw', encoder.encode(password), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveBits']
  );
  const bits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits({
    name: 'PBKDF2',
    salt: encoder.encode(salt),
    iterations: 100000,
    hash: 'SHA-256'
  }, key, 256);

  const hash = Array.from(new Uint8Array(bits), b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  return {salt, hash};
}

async function verifyPassword(password, salt, expected) {
  const result = await hashPassword(password, salt);
  return result.hash === expected;
}

function getCookie(request, name) {
  const cookie = request.headers.get('Cookie') || '';
  const match = cookie.split(';').map(v => v.trim()).find(v => v.startsWith(name + '='));
  return match ? decodeURIComponent(match.slice(name.length + 1)) : null;
}

function sessionCookie(token, maxAge = 2592000) {
  return `korean_session=${token}; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=${maxAge}`;
}

function clearSessionCookie() {
  return 'korean_session=; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=0';
}

function sameOrigin(request) {
  const origin = request.headers.get('Origin');
  if (!origin) return true;
  try {
    return new URL(origin).host === new URL(request.url).host;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
return { randomToken, sha256, hashPassword, verifyPassword, getCookie, sessionCookie, clearSessionCookie, sameOrigin };
})();

// ┌─ module: database ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.database = (() => {
function requireDB(env) {
  if (!env.DB) throw new Error('DATABASE_NOT_CONFIGURED');
  return env.DB;
}

async function getUserById(db, id) {
  return db.prepare(`
    SELECT
      u.id,
      u.username,
      u.display_name AS displayName,
      u.avatar,
      u.balance,
      u.created_at AS createdAt,
      COALESCE(p.bio, '') AS bio,
      COALESCE(p.role, 'player') AS role,
      COALESCE(p.level, 1) AS level,
      COALESCE(p.xp, 0) AS xp,
      COALESCE(p.xp_next, 100) AS xpNext,
      COALESCE(p.stars, 0) AS stars,
      COALESCE(p.messages, 0) AS messages,
      COALESCE(p.reactions, 0) AS reactions,
      COALESCE(p.voice_messages, 0) AS voiceMessages,
      COALESCE(p.memberships, 0) AS memberships,
      COALESCE(p.warnings, 0) AS warnings,
      COALESCE(p.banner, 'default') AS banner,
      COALESCE(p.background, 'default') AS background,
      CASE WHEN p.background LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(p.background, 5) END AS avatarImage,
      CASE WHEN p.banner LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(p.banner, 5) END AS bannerImage
    FROM users u
    LEFT JOIN player_profiles p ON p.user_id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = ?
  `).bind(id).first();
}
return { requireDB, getUserById };
})();

// ┌─ module: extras ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.extras = (() => {
const { getCookie, sha256 } = __M.security;
const { requireDB, getUserById } = __M.database;

const json = (d, s = 200, h = {}) => new Response(JSON.stringify(d), {
  status: s, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', ...h }
});

const EMOJIS = ['👍', '❤️', '😂', '🔥', '😮', '😢', '👏', '🎉'];
const MIMES = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp', 'image/gif', 'audio/webm', 'audio/ogg', 'audio/mp4', 'audio/mpeg'];
const ID_RE = /^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i;

async function sessionUser(request, db) {
  const token = getCookie(request, 'korean_session');
  if (!token) return null;
  const row = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT user_id FROM sessions
    WHERE token_hash = ? AND julianday(expires_at) > julianday('now')
  `).bind(await sha256(token)).first();
  return row?.user_id || null;
}

async function attachReactions(db, userId, messages) {
  for (const m of messages) m.reactions = [];
  if (!messages.length) return messages;
  try {
    const map = new Map();
    for (let i = 0; i < messages.length; i += 80) {
      const part = messages.slice(i, i + 80).map(m => m.id);
      const rows = await db.prepare(`
        SELECT message_id AS id, emoji, COUNT(*) AS count, MAX(user_id = ?) AS mine
        FROM message_reactions
        WHERE message_id IN (${part.map(() => '?').join(',')})
        GROUP BY message_id, emoji ORDER BY MIN(created_at)
      `).bind(userId, ...part).all();
      for (const r of rows.results || []) {
        if (!map.has(r.id)) map.set(r.id, []);
        map.get(r.id).push({ emoji: r.emoji, count: r.count, mine: !!r.mine });
      }
    }
    for (const m of messages) m.reactions = map.get(m.id) || [];
  } catch (e) { console.error('REACTIONS_UNAVAILABLE', e?.message); }
  return messages;
}

async function accessInfo(db, userId, messageId) {
  if (!ID_RE.test(messageId)) return null;
  const m = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, group_id, private_chat_id, sender_id, body, deleted_for_everyone_at
    FROM messages WHERE id = ?
  `).bind(messageId).first();
  if (!m || m.deleted_for_everyone_at) return null;
  if (m.group_id) {
    const gm = await db.prepare(
      'SELECT role FROM group_members WHERE group_id = ? AND user_id = ?'
    ).bind(m.group_id, userId).first();
    return gm ? { m, role: gm.role } : null;
  }
  if (m.private_chat_id) {
    const c = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT 1 AS x FROM private_chats
      WHERE id = ? AND (user_low_id = ? OR user_high_id = ?)
    `).bind(m.private_chat_id, userId, userId).first();
    return c ? { m, role: 'member' } : null;
  }
  return null;
}

const isTyped = body => String(body).trimStart().startsWith('{"type"');

// Typed (JSON) message bodies are only allowed for images the sender uploaded.
// This also blocks forged "core_gift" cards typed by hand.
async function validateTypedBody(db, userId, text) {
  if (!isTyped(text)) return { ok: true, text };
  let parsed;
  try { parsed = JSON.parse(text); } catch { return { ok: false }; }
  const id = String(parsed?.id || '');
  if (!['kc_image', 'kc_voice'].includes(parsed?.type) || !ID_RE.test(id)) return { ok: false };

  const row = await db.prepare('SELECT mime FROM uploads WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?').bind(id, userId).first();
  if (!row) return { ok: false };

  if (parsed.type === 'kc_voice') {
    if (!row.mime.startsWith('audio/')) return { ok: false };
    return { ok: true, text: JSON.stringify({
      type: 'kc_voice', id, dur: Math.max(0, Math.min(300, Math.round(Number(parsed.dur) || 0)))
    }) };
  }
  if (!row.mime.startsWith('image/')) return { ok: false };
  return { ok: true, text: JSON.stringify({
    type: 'kc_image', id, caption: String(parsed.caption || '').slice(0, 300)
  }) };
}

async function upload(request, db, userId) {
  const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
  const mime = String(body?.mime || '');
  const data = String(body?.data || '');

  if (!MIMES.includes(mime) || !data || data.length > 900000 || !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$/.test(data)) {
    return json({ error: 'تصویر نامعتبر است یا از حد مجاز (حدود ۶۵۰ کیلوبایت) بزرگ‌تر است.' }, 400);
  }

  let head;
  try { head = Array.from(atob(data.slice(0, 24)), c => c.charCodeAt(0)); }
  catch { return json({ error: 'فایل تصویر معتبر نیست.' }, 400); }

  const ascii = head.slice(8, 12).map(c => String.fromCharCode(c)).join('');
  const ok =
    (mime === 'image/jpeg' && head[0] === 0xFF && head[1] === 0xD8 && head[2] === 0xFF) ||
    (mime === 'image/png' && head[0] === 0x89 && head[1] === 0x50 && head[2] === 0x4E && head[3] === 0x47) ||
    (mime === 'image/gif' && head[0] === 0x47 && head[1] === 0x49 && head[2] === 0x46) ||
    (mime === 'image/webp' && head[0] === 0x52 && head[1] === 0x49 && ascii === 'WEBP') ||
    (mime === 'audio/webm' && head[0] === 0x1A && head[1] === 0x45 && head[2] === 0xDF && head[3] === 0xA3) ||
    (mime === 'audio/ogg' && head[0] === 0x4F && head[1] === 0x67 && head[2] === 0x67 && head[3] === 0x53) ||
    (mime === 'audio/mp4' && head.slice(4, 8).map(c => String.fromCharCode(c)).join('') === 'ftyp') ||
    (mime === 'audio/mpeg' && ((head[0] === 0x49 && head[1] === 0x44 && head[2] === 0x33) || (head[0] === 0xFF && (head[1] & 0xE0) === 0xE0)));

  if (!ok) return json({ error: 'محتوای فایل با نوع تصویر مطابقت ندارد.' }, 400);

  const recent = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM uploads
    WHERE user_id = ? AND created_at > datetime('now','-1 hour')
  `).bind(userId).first();
  if ((recent?.c || 0) >= 40) return json({ error: 'تعداد آپلودهای ساعت اخیر زیاد است؛ کمی بعد تلاش کن.' }, 429);

  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
  await db.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO uploads (id, user_id, mime, size, data) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  `).bind(id, userId, mime, Math.floor(data.length * 3 / 4), data).run();

  return json({ id, url: '/api/img/' + id }, 201);
}

async function serveImage(request, db, id) {
  if (!ID_RE.test(id)) return json({ error: 'فایل پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
  const row = await db.prepare('SELECT mime, data FROM uploads WHERE id = ?').bind(id).first();
  if (!row) return json({ error: 'فایل پیدا نشد.' }, 404);

  const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(row.data), c => c.charCodeAt(0));
  const headers = {
    'Content-Type': row.mime,
    'Cache-Control': 'private, max-age=31536000, immutable',
    'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
    'Content-Security-Policy': "default-src 'none'; sandbox",
    'Accept-Ranges': 'bytes'
  };

  const range = /^bytes=(\d*)-(\d*)$/.exec(request.headers.get('Range') || '');
  if (range && (range[1] || range[2])) {
    let start = range[1] ? Number(range[1]) : Math.max(0, bytes.length - Number(range[2]));
    let end = range[1] && range[2] ? Number(range[2]) : bytes.length - 1;
    end = Math.min(end, bytes.length - 1);
    if (start > end || start >= bytes.length) {
      return new Response(null, { status: 416, headers: { ...headers, 'Content-Range': `bytes */${bytes.length}` } });
    }
    return new Response(bytes.slice(start, end + 1), { status: 206, headers: {
      ...headers, 'Content-Range': `bytes ${start}-${end}/${bytes.length}`, 'Content-Length': String(end - start + 1)
    } });
  }
  return new Response(bytes, { headers });
}

async function profileImage(request, db, userId) {
  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const kind = body.kind === 'banner' ? 'banner' : body.kind === 'avatar' ? 'avatar' : null;
  if (!kind) return json({ error: 'نوع تصویر معتبر نیست.' }, 400);

  let value = 'default';
  if (body.id) {
    const id = String(body.id);
    const own = ID_RE.test(id) && await db.prepare(
      'SELECT 1 AS x FROM uploads WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?'
    ).bind(id, userId).first();
    if (!own) return json({ error: 'تصویر پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
    value = 'img:' + id;
  }

  await db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO player_profiles (user_id) VALUES (?)').bind(userId).run();
  // avatar photo is kept in player_profiles.background, banner in player_profiles.banner
  const column = kind === 'banner' ? 'banner' : 'background';
  await db.prepare(`UPDATE player_profiles SET ${column} = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE user_id = ?`)
    .bind(value, userId).run();

  return json({ message: 'تصویر ذخیره شد.', user: await getUserById(db, userId) });
}

async function react(request, db, userId) {
  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const emoji = String(body.emoji || '');
  if (!EMOJIS.includes(emoji)) return json({ error: 'ری‌اکشن معتبر نیست.' }, 400);

  const info = await accessInfo(db, userId, String(body.messageId || ''));
  if (!info) return json({ error: 'پیام پیدا نشد.' }, 404);

  const had = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT 1 AS x FROM message_reactions WHERE message_id = ? AND user_id = ? AND emoji = ?
  `).bind(info.m.id, userId, emoji).first();

  if (had) {
    await db.batch([
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM message_reactions WHERE message_id = ? AND user_id = ? AND emoji = ?')
        .bind(info.m.id, userId, emoji),
      db.prepare('UPDATE player_profiles SET reactions = MAX(0, reactions - 1) WHERE user_id = ?')
        .bind(info.m.sender_id)
    ]);
  } else {
    await db.batch([
      db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO message_reactions (message_id, user_id, emoji) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
        .bind(info.m.id, userId, emoji),
      db.prepare('UPDATE player_profiles SET reactions = reactions + 1 WHERE user_id = ?')
        .bind(info.m.sender_id)
    ]);
  }
  return json({ message: had ? 'ری‌اکشن برداشته شد.' : 'ری‌اکشن ثبت شد.' });
}

async function editOrDelete(request, db, userId, messageId) {
  const info = await accessInfo(db, userId, messageId);
  if (!info) return json({ error: 'پیام پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
  const { m, role } = info;

  if (request.method === 'PATCH') {
    if (m.sender_id !== userId) return json({ error: 'فقط پیام‌های خودت را می‌توانی ویرایش کنی.' }, 403);
    if (isTyped(m.body)) return json({ error: 'این نوع پیام قابل ویرایش نیست.' }, 400);
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const text = String(body.text || '').trim();
    if (!text || text.length > 4000 || isTyped(text)) return json({ error: 'متن پیام معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
    await db.prepare(`UPDATE messages SET body = ?, edited_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?`).bind(text, m.id).run();
    return json({ message: 'پیام ویرایش شد.' });
  }

  if (request.method === 'DELETE') {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (body.mode === 'me') {
      await db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO message_hidden (message_id, user_id) VALUES (?, ?)').bind(m.id, userId).run();
      return json({ message: 'پیام فقط برای تو پاک شد.' });
    }
    const moderator = m.group_id && ['owner', 'admin'].includes(role);
    if (m.sender_id !== userId && !moderator) return json({ error: 'اجازه‌ی حذف این پیام را نداری.' }, 403);
    await db.prepare(`UPDATE messages SET deleted_for_everyone_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?`).bind(m.id).run();
    return json({ message: 'پیام برای همه پاک شد.' });
  }
  return json({ error: 'روش درخواست پشتیبانی نمی‌شود.' }, 405);
}

async function daily(request, db, userId) {
  const row = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT streak, last_claim, (last_claim = date('now')) AS today,
           (last_claim = date('now','-1 day')) AS yesterday
    FROM daily_claims WHERE user_id = ?
  `).bind(userId).first();

  if (request.method === 'GET') {
    const live = row && (row.today || row.yesterday);
    return json({ available: !row?.today, streak: live ? row.streak : 0 });
  }

  const claim = await db.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO daily_claims (user_id, last_claim, streak) VALUES (?, date('now'), 1)
    ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET
      streak = CASE WHEN last_claim = date('now','-1 day') THEN streak + 1 ELSE 1 END,
      last_claim = date('now')
    WHERE last_claim < date('now')
  `).bind(userId).run();

  if (!claim.meta?.changes) return json({ error: 'جایزه‌ی امروزت را قبلاً گرفته‌ای. فردا برگرد!' }, 400);

  const { streak } = await db.prepare('SELECT streak FROM daily_claims WHERE user_id = ?').bind(userId).first();
  const coins = 100000 * Math.min(streak, 7);
  const stars = streak % 7 === 0 ? 5 : 0;

  await db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO player_profiles (user_id) VALUES (?)').bind(userId).run();
  const p = await db.prepare('SELECT level, xp, xp_next FROM player_profiles WHERE user_id = ?').bind(userId).first();
  let { level, xp, xp_next } = p;
  xp += 20;
  while (xp >= xp_next) { xp -= xp_next; level += 1; xp_next = Math.floor(xp_next * 1.25); }

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare('UPDATE users SET balance = balance + ? WHERE id = ?').bind(coins, userId),
    db.prepare('UPDATE player_profiles SET stars = stars + ?, level = ?, xp = ?, xp_next = ? WHERE user_id = ?')
      .bind(stars, level, xp, xp_next, userId)
  ]);

  return json({ message: `جایزه‌ی روز ${streak}: ${coins.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} سکه + ۲۰ XP` + (stars ? ' + ۵ ستاره 🌟' : ''), streak, coins, stars });
}

async function leaderboard(request, db, userId) {
  const by = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('by') || 'coins';
  const order = {
    coins: 'u.balance DESC',
    level: 'COALESCE(p.level,1) DESC, COALESCE(p.xp,0) DESC',
    messages: 'COALESCE(p.messages,0) DESC'
  }[by] || 'u.balance DESC';

  const rows = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT u.id, u.username, u.display_name AS displayName, u.avatar, u.balance,
           CASE WHEN p.background LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(p.background,5) END AS avatarImage,
           COALESCE(p.level,1) AS level, COALESCE(p.messages,0) AS messages,
           COALESCE(p.role,'player') AS role
    FROM users u LEFT JOIN player_profiles p ON p.user_id = u.id
    WHERE COALESCE(p.role,'player') <> 'owner'
    ORDER BY ${order} LIMIT 25
  `).all();

  return json({ by, me: userId, users: rows.results || [] });
}

async function scopeAccess(db, userId, scope, id) {
  if (!ID_RE.test(id)) return null;
  if (scope === 'group') {
    const gm = await db.prepare('SELECT role FROM group_members WHERE group_id = ? AND user_id = ?').bind(id, userId).first();
    return gm ? { role: gm.role } : null;
  }
  if (scope === 'private') {
    const c = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT user_low_id, user_high_id FROM private_chats
      WHERE id = ? AND (user_low_id = ? OR user_high_id = ?)
    `).bind(id, userId, userId).first();
    return c ? { peer: c.user_low_id === userId ? c.user_high_id : c.user_low_id } : null;
  }
  return null;
}

const touchPresence = (db, userId) => db.prepare(`
  INSERT INTO user_presence (user_id, last_seen) VALUES (?, datetime('now'))
  ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET last_seen = datetime('now')
`).bind(userId).run();

async function typing(request, db, userId) {
  if (request.method === 'POST') {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const scope = body.scope === 'private' ? 'private' : 'group';
    if (!await scopeAccess(db, userId, scope, String(body.id || ''))) return json({ error: 'دسترسی نداری.' }, 403);
    await db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO typing_status (scope, scope_id, user_id, at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
      ON CONFLICT(scope, scope_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET at = datetime('now')
    `).bind(scope, body.id, userId).run();
    return json({ ok: true });
  }

  const q = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
  const scope = q.get('scope') === 'private' ? 'private' : 'group';
  const id = q.get('id') || '';
  const access = await scopeAccess(db, userId, scope, id);
  if (!access) return json({ error: 'دسترسی نداری.' }, 403);

  const out = { typing: [], peer: null, pin: null };

  const rows = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT u.display_name AS name FROM typing_status t JOIN users u ON u.id = t.user_id
    WHERE t.scope = ? AND t.scope_id = ? AND t.user_id <> ? AND t.at > datetime('now','-6 seconds')
  `).bind(scope, id, userId).all();
  out.typing = (rows.results || []).map(r => r.name);

  if (scope === 'private') {
    const p = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT (julianday('now') - julianday(last_seen)) * 86400 AS ago FROM user_presence WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(access.peer).first();
    out.peer = p ? { online: p.ago < 75, agoSec: Math.round(p.ago) } : { online: false, agoSec: null };
  } else {
    out.pin = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT pm.message_id AS id, m.body, u.display_name AS displayName
      FROM pinned_messages pm
      JOIN messages m ON m.id = pm.message_id AND m.deleted_for_everyone_at IS NULL
      JOIN users u ON u.id = m.sender_id
      WHERE pm.group_id = ?
    `).bind(id).first() || null;
  }
  return json(out);
}

async function pin(request, db, userId) {
  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const groupId = String(body.groupId || '');
  const access = await scopeAccess(db, userId, 'group', groupId);
  if (!access) return json({ error: 'دسترسی نداری.' }, 403);
  if (!['owner', 'admin'].includes(access.role)) return json({ error: 'فقط مدیران گروه می‌توانند پیام را سنجاق کنند.' }, 403);

  if (body.action === 'unpin') {
    await db.prepare('DELETE FROM pinned_messages WHERE group_id = ?').bind(groupId).run();
    return json({ message: 'سنجاق برداشته شد.' });
  }
  const mid = String(body.messageId || '');
  const ok = ID_RE.test(mid) && await db.prepare(
    'SELECT 1 AS x FROM messages WHERE id = ? AND group_id = ? AND deleted_for_everyone_at IS NULL'
  ).bind(mid, groupId).first();
  if (!ok) return json({ error: 'پیام پیدا نشد.' }, 404);

  await db.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO pinned_messages (group_id, message_id, pinned_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?)
    ON CONFLICT(group_id) DO UPDATE SET message_id = excluded.message_id, pinned_by = excluded.pinned_by, created_at = datetime('now')
  `).bind(groupId, mid, userId).run();
  return json({ message: 'پیام سنجاق شد 📌' });
}

const isExtrasPath = path =>
  path === '/api/upload' || path.startsWith('/api/img/') || path === '/api/profile/image' ||
  path === '/api/core/reactions' || path.startsWith('/api/core/messages/') ||
  path === '/api/core/daily' || path === '/api/core/leaderboard' ||
  path === '/api/core/ping' || path === '/api/core/typing' || path === '/api/core/pin';

async function handleExtras(request, env, path) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const userId = await sessionUser(request, db);
  if (!userId) return json({ error: 'ابتدا وارد حساب شو.' }, 401);

  const m = request.method;
  if (path === '/api/upload' && m === 'POST') return upload(request, db, userId);
  if (path.startsWith('/api/img/') && m === 'GET') return serveImage(request, db, path.slice(9));
  if (path === '/api/profile/image' && m === 'POST') return profileImage(request, db, userId);
  if (path === '/api/core/reactions' && m === 'POST') return react(request, db, userId);
  if (path === '/api/core/daily' && (m === 'GET' || m === 'POST')) return daily(request, db, userId);
  if (path === '/api/core/leaderboard' && m === 'GET') return leaderboard(request, db, userId);
  if (path === '/api/core/ping' && m === 'POST') { await touchPresence(db, userId); return json({ ok: true }); }
  if (path === '/api/core/typing' && (m === 'GET' || m === 'POST')) return typing(request, db, userId);
  if (path === '/api/core/pin' && m === 'POST') return pin(request, db, userId);
  if (path.startsWith('/api/core/messages/'))
    return editOrDelete(request, db, userId, path.slice('/api/core/messages/'.length));

  return json({ error: 'مسیر پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
}
return { attachReactions, validateTypedBody, isExtrasPath, handleExtras };
})();

const MIGRATIONS = [
  ['0000_base', base], ['0001_core_tables', m0001], ['0002_core_features', m0002],
  ['0003_support_tickets', m0003], ['0004_seed_shop', m0004],
  ['0005_uploads_reactions_daily', m0005], ['0006_presence_typing_pins', m0006]
];

const split = sql => String(sql)
  .split('\n').filter(l => !l.trim().startsWith('--')).join('\n')
  .split(/;\s*(?:\r?\n|$)/)
  .map(s => s.trim())
  .filter(s => s && !/^PRAGMA/i.test(s));

let ready = null;

async function run(db) {
  await db.prepare(
    'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS kc_migrations (name TEXT PRIMARY KEY, applied_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime(\'now\')))'
  ).run();

  const done = new Set(((await db.prepare('SELECT name FROM kc_migrations').all()).results || []).map(r => r.name));

  for (const [name, sql] of MIGRATIONS) {
    if (done.has(name)) continue;
    let failed = false;
    for (const statement of split(sql)) {
      try { await db.prepare(statement).run(); }
      catch (e) {
        if (!/already exists|duplicate column/i.test(String(e?.message))) {
          failed = true;
          console.error('MIGRATION_STATEMENT_FAILED', name, e?.message);
        }
      }
    }
    if (!failed) await db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO kc_migrations (name) VALUES (?)').bind(name).run();
  }
}

function ensureSchema(env) {
  if (!env?.DB) return Promise.resolve();
  if (!ready) ready = run(env.DB).catch(e => { console.error('BOOTSTRAP_FAILED', e?.message); ready = null; });
  return ready;
}
return { ensureSchema };
})();

// ┌─ module: auth ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.auth = (() => {
const {
  randomToken, sha256, hashPassword, verifyPassword,
  getCookie, sessionCookie, clearSessionCookie
} = __M.security;

const {getUserById, requireDB} = __M.database;

const json = (data, status = 200, headers = {}) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    status,
    headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json; charset=utf-8', ...headers}
  });

async function createSession(db, userId) {
  const token = randomToken(32);
  const tokenHash = await sha256(token);
  const expires = new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 86400000).toISOString();

  await db.prepare(
    'INSERT INTO sessions (id, user_id, token_hash, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)'
  ).bind(crypto.randomUUID(), userId, tokenHash, expires).run();

  return token;
}

async function getSessionUserId(request, db) {
  const token = getCookie(request, 'korean_session');
  if (!token) return null;

  const tokenHash = await sha256(token);
  const session = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT user_id
    FROM sessions
    WHERE token_hash = ?
      AND julianday(expires_at) > julianday('now')
  `).bind(tokenHash).first();

  return session?.user_id || null;
}

async function register(request, env) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));

  const username = String(body.username || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const displayName = String(body.displayName || username).trim().slice(0, 20);
  const password = String(body.password || '');
  const avatar = Number(body.avatar);

  if (!/^[a-z0-9_]{7,24}$/.test(username))
    return json({error:'نام کاربری باید ۷ تا ۲۴ حرف انگلیسی، عدد یا _ باشد.'}, 400);

  if (password.length < 8 || password.length > 128)
    return json({error:'رمز عبور باید بین ۸ تا ۱۲۸ کاراکتر باشد.'}, 400);

  if (!Number.isInteger(avatar) || avatar < 0 || avatar > 47)
    return json({error:'آواتار انتخاب‌شده معتبر نیست.'}, 400);

  const exists = await db.prepare(
    'SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?'
  ).bind(username).first();

  if (exists) return json({error:'این نام کاربری قبلاً ثبت شده است.'}, 409);

  const {salt, hash} = await hashPassword(password);
  const userId = crypto.randomUUID();

  try {
    await db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO users
        (id, username, display_name, password_hash, password_salt, avatar, balance)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1000000)
    `).bind(userId, username, displayName || username, hash, salt, avatar).run();

    await db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR IGNORE INTO player_profiles (user_id)
      VALUES (?)
    `).bind(userId).run();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('REGISTER_FAILED:', error);
    return json({error:'ساخت حساب انجام نشد؛ دوباره تلاش کن.'}, 500);
  }

  const token = await createSession(db, userId);
  const user = await getUserById(db, userId);

  return json(
    {message:'حساب ساخته شد!', user},
    201,
    {'Set-Cookie':sessionCookie(token)}
  );
}

async function login(request, env) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));

  const username = String(body.username || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const password = String(body.password || '');

  const account = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, password_hash, password_salt
    FROM users
    WHERE username = ?
  `).bind(username).first();

  if (!account || !(await verifyPassword(
    password, account.password_salt, account.password_hash
  ))) {
    return json({error:'نام کاربری یا رمز عبور اشتباه است.'}, 401);
  }

  await db.prepare(`
    INSERT OR IGNORE INTO player_profiles (user_id)
    VALUES (?)
  `).bind(account.id).run();

  const token = await createSession(db, account.id);
  const user = await getUserById(db, account.id);

  return json(
    {message:'با موفقیت وارد شدی!', user},
    200,
    {'Set-Cookie':sessionCookie(token)}
  );
}

async function me(request, env) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const userId = await getSessionUserId(request, db);

  if (!userId) return json({user:null});

  const user = await getUserById(db, userId);
  return json({user});
}

async function updateProfile(request, env) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const userId = await getSessionUserId(request, db);

  if (!userId) return json({error:'ابتدا وارد حساب شو.'}, 401);

  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));

  const displayName = String(body.displayName || '').trim();
  const bio = String(body.bio || '').trim();
  const avatar = Number(body.avatar);

  if (displayName.length < 2 || displayName.length > 20)
    return json({error:'نام نمایشی باید بین ۲ تا ۲۰ کاراکتر باشد.'}, 400);

  if (bio.length > 160)
    return json({error:'بیوگرافی حداکثر ۱۶۰ کاراکتر است.'}, 400);

  if (!Number.isInteger(avatar) || avatar < 0 || avatar > 47)
    return json({error:'آواتار معتبر نیست.'}, 400);

  await db.prepare(`
    UPDATE users
    SET display_name = ?, avatar = ?
    WHERE id = ?
  `).bind(displayName, avatar, userId).run();

  await db.prepare(`
    INSERT OR IGNORE INTO player_profiles (user_id)
    VALUES (?)
  `).bind(userId).run();

  await db.prepare(`
    UPDATE player_profiles
    SET bio = ?, updated_at = datetime('now')
    WHERE user_id = ?
  `).bind(bio, userId).run();

  return json({
    message:'پروفایل با موفقیت ذخیره شد.',
    user:await getUserById(db, userId)
  });
}

async function logout(request, env) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const token = getCookie(request, 'korean_session');

  if (token) {
    const tokenHash = await sha256(token);
    await db.prepare(
      'DELETE FROM sessions WHERE token_hash = ?'
    ).bind(tokenHash).run();
  }

  return json(
    {message:'خارج شدی.'},
    200,
    {'Set-Cookie':clearSessionCookie()}
  );
}
return { register, login, me, updateProfile, logout };
})();

// ┌─ module: social ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.social = (() => {
const {getCookie, sha256} = __M.security;
const {requireDB} = __M.database;
const {attachReactions, validateTypedBody} = __M.extras;

const json = (data, status = 200) => new Response(
  JSON.stringify(data),
  {
    status,
    headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json; charset=utf-8'}
  }
);

async function getUserId(request, db) {
  const token = getCookie(request, 'korean_session');
  if (!token) return null;

  const hash = await sha256(token);

  const session = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT user_id
    FROM sessions
    WHERE token_hash = ?
      AND julianday(expires_at) > julianday('now')
  `).bind(hash).first();

  return session?.user_id || null;
}

async function requireMembership(db, groupId, userId) {
  return await db.prepare(`
    SELECT role, muted_until
    FROM group_members
    WHERE group_id = ? AND user_id = ?
  `).bind(groupId, userId).first();
}

async function listGroups(db, userId) {
  const result = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT
      g.id,
      g.name,
      g.description,
      g.owner_id AS ownerId,
      g.is_official AS isOfficial,
      g.join_approval AS joinApproval,
      g.created_at AS createdAt,
      gm.role AS myRole,
      CASE WHEN gm.user_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END AS isMember,
      (
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM group_members members
        WHERE members.group_id = g.id
      ) AS memberCount,
      (
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM messages m
        WHERE m.group_id = g.id
          AND gm.user_id IS NOT NULL
          AND m.sender_id <> ?
          AND m.created_at > COALESCE(gm.last_read_at, '1970-01-01 00:00:00')
          AND m.deleted_for_everyone_at IS NULL
          AND NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM message_hidden h
            WHERE h.message_id = m.id AND h.user_id = ?
          )
      ) AS unreadCount
    FROM groups g
    LEFT JOIN group_members gm
      ON gm.group_id = g.id AND gm.user_id = ?
    ORDER BY g.is_official DESC, g.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 100
  `).bind(userId, userId, userId).all();

  return result.results || [];
}

async function createGroup(request, db, userId) {
  let body;

  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return json({error:'اطلاعات ارسالی معتبر نیست.'}, 400);
  }

  const name = String(body.name || '').trim();
  const description = String(body.description || '').trim();

  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 50) {
    return json({error:'نام گروه باید بین ۲ تا ۵۰ کاراکتر باشد.'}, 400);
  }

  if (description.length > 500) {
    return json({error:'توضیحات گروه حداکثر ۵۰۰ کاراکتر است.'}, 400);
  }

  const approval = body.joinApproval === true ? 1 : 0;
  const id = crypto.randomUUID();

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO groups
      (id, name, description, owner_id, join_approval)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).bind(id, name, description, userId, approval),

    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO group_members
      (group_id, user_id, role)
      VALUES (?, ?, 'owner')
    `).bind(id, userId),

    db.prepare(`
      UPDATE player_profiles
      SET memberships = memberships + 1
      WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(userId)
  ]);

  return json({
    message:'گروه با موفقیت ساخته شد.',
    group:{
      id,
      name,
      description,
      ownerId:userId,
      isOfficial:0,
      joinApproval:approval,
      myRole:'owner',
      isMember:1,
      memberCount:1
    }
  }, 201);
}

async function joinGroup(db, groupId, userId) {
  const group = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, join_approval
    FROM groups
    WHERE id = ?
  `).bind(groupId).first();

  if (!group) return json({error:'گروه پیدا نشد.'}, 404);

  const existing = await requireMembership(db, groupId, userId);

  if (existing) {
    return json({message:'عضو این گروه هستی.', isMember:true});
  }

  const ban = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT 1
    FROM group_bans
    WHERE group_id = ? AND user_id = ?
      AND (
        expires_at IS NULL
        OR julianday(expires_at) > julianday('now')
      )
  `).bind(groupId, userId).first();

  if (ban) {
    return json({error:'عضویت شما در این گروه محدود شده است.'}, 403);
  }

  if (group.join_approval) {
    await db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO group_join_requests (id, group_id, user_id, status)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'pending')
      ON CONFLICT(group_id, user_id)
      DO UPDATE SET status = 'pending',
                    reviewed_by = NULL,
                    reviewed_at = NULL,
                    created_at = datetime('now')
    `).bind(crypto.randomUUID(), groupId, userId).run();

    return json({
      message:'درخواست عضویت ارسال شد و منتظر تأیید مدیر گروه است.',
      pending:true
    }, 202);
  }

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR IGNORE INTO group_members (group_id, user_id)
      VALUES (?, ?)
    `).bind(groupId, userId),

    db.prepare(`
      UPDATE player_profiles
      SET memberships = memberships + 1
      WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(userId)
  ]);

  return json({message:'با موفقیت عضو گروه شدی.', isMember:true});
}

async function leaveGroup(db, groupId, userId) {
  const membership = await requireMembership(db, groupId, userId);

  if (!membership) {
    return json({error:'عضو این گروه نیستی.'}, 404);
  }

  if (membership.role === 'owner') {
    return json({
      error:'مالک گروه نمی‌تواند مستقیماً خارج شود. ابتدا باید مالکیت را منتقل کند یا گروه را حذف کند.'
    }, 403);
  }

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare(`
      DELETE FROM group_members
      WHERE group_id = ? AND user_id = ?
    `).bind(groupId, userId),

    db.prepare(`
      UPDATE player_profiles
      SET memberships = MAX(0, memberships - 1)
      WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(userId)
  ]);

  return json({message:'از گروه خارج شدی.'});
}

async function getMessages(db, groupId, userId) {
  const membership = await requireMembership(db, groupId, userId);

  if (!membership) {
    return json({error:'برای دیدن پیام‌ها باید عضو گروه باشی.'}, 403);
  }

  const result = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT * FROM (
      SELECT
        m.id,
        m.sender_id AS senderId,
        u.username,
        u.display_name AS displayName,
        u.avatar,
        CASE WHEN pp.background LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(pp.background, 5) END AS avatarImage,
        m.body,
        m.reply_to AS replyTo,
        (SELECT r.body FROM messages r WHERE r.id = m.reply_to AND r.deleted_for_everyone_at IS NULL) AS replyBody,
        (SELECT ru.display_name FROM messages r JOIN users ru ON ru.id = r.sender_id WHERE r.id = m.reply_to) AS replyName,
        m.forwarded_from AS forwardedFrom,
        m.edited_at AS editedAt,
        m.created_at AS createdAt
      FROM messages m
      JOIN users u ON u.id = m.sender_id
      LEFT JOIN player_profiles pp ON pp.user_id = u.id
      WHERE m.group_id = ?
        AND m.deleted_for_everyone_at IS NULL
        AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM message_hidden h
          WHERE h.message_id = m.id AND h.user_id = ?
        )
      ORDER BY m.created_at DESC, m.id DESC
      LIMIT 300
    ) ORDER BY createdAt ASC, id ASC
  `).bind(groupId, userId).all();

  await db.prepare(`
    UPDATE group_members
    SET last_read_at = strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f','now')
    WHERE group_id = ? AND user_id = ?
  `).bind(groupId, userId).run();

  return json({
    messages: await attachReactions(db, userId, result.results || [])
  });
}

async function sendMessage(request, db, groupId, userId) {
  const membership = await requireMembership(db, groupId, userId);

  if (!membership) {
    return json({error:'ابتدا باید عضو گروه شوی.'}, 403);
  }

  if (
    membership.muted_until &&
    new Date(membership.muted_until).getTime() > Date.now()
  ) {
    return json({error:'امکان ارسال پیام برای شما موقتاً غیرفعال است.'}, 403);
  }

  let body;

  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return json({error:'اطلاعات پیام معتبر نیست.'}, 400);
  }

  let text = String(body.text || '').trim();

  if (!text || text.length > 4000) {
    return json({error:'پیام باید بین ۱ تا ۴۰۰۰ کاراکتر باشد.'}, 400);
  }

  const typed = await validateTypedBody(db, userId, text);
  if (!typed.ok) return json({error:'نوع پیام مجاز نیست.'}, 400);
  text = typed.text;

  const id = crypto.randomUUID();

  const replyTo = body.replyTo
    ? String(body.replyTo)
    : null;

  if (replyTo) {
    const parent = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT id
      FROM messages
      WHERE id = ? AND group_id = ?
        AND deleted_for_everyone_at IS NULL
    `).bind(replyTo, groupId).first();

    if (!parent) {
      return json({error:'پیام موردنظر برای پاسخ پیدا نشد.'}, 400);
    }
  }

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO messages
      (id, group_id, sender_id, body, reply_to, created_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f','now'))
    `).bind(id, groupId, userId, text, replyTo),

    db.prepare(`
      UPDATE player_profiles
      SET messages = messages + 1
      WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(userId),

    db.prepare(`
      UPDATE groups
      SET updated_at = datetime('now')
      WHERE id = ?
    `).bind(groupId)
  ]);

  const message = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT
      m.id,
      m.sender_id AS senderId,
      u.username,
      u.display_name AS displayName,
      u.avatar,
      m.body,
      m.reply_to AS replyTo,
      m.created_at AS createdAt
    FROM messages m
    JOIN users u ON u.id = m.sender_id
    WHERE m.id = ?
  `).bind(id).first();

  return json({message}, 201);
}

async function handleSocial(request, env, path) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const userId = await getUserId(request, db);

  if (!userId) {
    return json({error:'برای استفاده از این بخش ابتدا وارد حساب شو.'}, 401);
  }

  const method = request.method;

  if (path === '/api/groups' && method === 'GET') {
    return json({groups:await listGroups(db, userId)});
  }

  if (path === '/api/groups' && method === 'POST') {
    return await createGroup(request, db, userId);
  }

  const match = path.match(
    /^\/api\/groups\/([a-f0-9-]+)(?:\/(join|leave|messages))?$/i
  );

  if (!match) {
    return json({error:'مسیر گروه معتبر نیست.'}, 404);
  }

  const groupId = match[1];
  const action = match[2] || '';

  if (action === 'join' && method === 'POST') {
    return await joinGroup(db, groupId, userId);
  }

  if (action === 'leave' && method === 'POST') {
    return await leaveGroup(db, groupId, userId);
  }

  if (action === 'messages' && method === 'GET') {
    return await getMessages(db, groupId, userId);
  }

  if (action === 'messages' && method === 'POST') {
    return await sendMessage(request, db, groupId, userId);
  }

  if (!action && method === 'GET') {
    const group = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT
        g.id,
        g.name,
        g.description,
        g.owner_id AS ownerId,
        g.is_official AS isOfficial,
        g.join_approval AS joinApproval,
        gm.role AS myRole,
        CASE WHEN gm.user_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END AS isMember,
        (
          SELECT COUNT(*) FROM group_members x
          WHERE x.group_id = g.id
        ) AS memberCount
      FROM groups g
      LEFT JOIN group_members gm
        ON gm.group_id = g.id AND gm.user_id = ?
      WHERE g.id = ?
    `).bind(userId, groupId).first();

    if (!group) return json({error:'گروه پیدا نشد.'}, 404);

    return json({group});
  }

  return json({error:'روش درخواست پشتیبانی نمی‌شود.'}, 405);
}
return { handleSocial };
})();

// ┌─ module: core ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.core = (() => {
const { getCookie, sha256 } = __M.security;
const { requireDB } = __M.database;
const { attachReactions, validateTypedBody } = __M.extras;

const json = (data, status = 200) => new Response(
  JSON.stringify(data),
  { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8' } }
);

const uid = () => crypto.randomUUID();

async function sessionUser(request, db) {
  const token = getCookie(request, 'korean_session');
  if (!token) return null;

  const hash = await sha256(token);
  const row = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT user_id FROM sessions
    WHERE token_hash = ?
      AND julianday(expires_at) > julianday('now')
  `).bind(hash).first();

  return row?.user_id || null;
}

async function getRole(db, userId) {
  const row = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT role FROM player_profiles WHERE user_id = ?
  `).bind(userId).first();

  return row?.role || 'player';
}

async function isOwner(db, userId) {
  return (await getRole(db, userId)) === 'owner';
}

async function blocked(db, a, b) {
  return !!await db.prepare(`
    SELECT 1 FROM user_blocks
    WHERE (blocker_id = ? AND blocked_user_id = ?)
       OR (blocker_id = ? AND blocked_user_id = ?)
  `).bind(a, b, b, a).first();
}

async function publicProfile(db, targetId) {
  return await db.prepare(`
    SELECT
      u.id,
      u.username,
      u.display_name AS displayName,
      u.avatar,
      u.balance,
      CASE WHEN p.background LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(p.background, 5) END AS avatarImage,
      CASE WHEN p.banner LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(p.banner, 5) END AS bannerImage,
      COALESCE(p.bio, '') AS bio,
      COALESCE(p.role, 'player') AS role,
      COALESCE(p.level, 1) AS level,
      COALESCE(p.xp, 0) AS xp,
      COALESCE(p.stars, 0) AS stars,
      COALESCE(p.messages, 0) AS messages,
      COALESCE(p.reactions, 0) AS reactions,
      COALESCE(p.memberships, 0) AS memberships
    FROM users u
    LEFT JOIN player_profiles p ON p.user_id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = ?
  `).bind(targetId).first();
}

async function createPrivateChat(db, userId, targetId) {
  if (!targetId || targetId === userId) {
    return { error: 'کاربر مقصد معتبر نیست.' };
  }

  const target = await publicProfile(db, targetId);
  if (!target) return { error: 'کاربر پیدا نشد.' };

  if (await blocked(db, userId, targetId)) {
    return { error: 'ارتباط با این کاربر امکان‌پذیر نیست.' };
  }

  const low = userId < targetId ? userId : targetId;
  const high = userId < targetId ? targetId : userId;

  await db.prepare(`
    INSERT OR IGNORE INTO private_chats
      (id, user_low_id, user_high_id)
    VALUES (?, ?, ?)
  `).bind(uid(), low, high).run();

  const chat = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT id FROM private_chats
    WHERE user_low_id = ? AND user_high_id = ?
  `).bind(low, high).first();

  return { chat };
}

async function getPrivateChat(db, userId, chatId) {
  return db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, user_low_id, user_high_id
    FROM private_chats
    WHERE id = ? AND (user_low_id = ? OR user_high_id = ?)
  `).bind(chatId, userId, userId).first();
}

async function privateMessages(request, db, userId, chatId) {
  const chat = await getPrivateChat(db, userId, chatId);

  if (!chat) return json({ error: 'گفتگو پیدا نشد.' }, 404);

  if (request.method === 'GET') {
    const result = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT * FROM (
        SELECT
          m.id,
          m.sender_id AS senderId,
          u.username,
          u.display_name AS displayName,
          u.avatar,
          CASE WHEN pp.background LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(pp.background, 5) END AS avatarImage,
          m.body,
          m.edited_at AS editedAt,
          m.created_at AS createdAt
        FROM messages m
        JOIN users u ON u.id = m.sender_id
        LEFT JOIN player_profiles pp ON pp.user_id = u.id
        WHERE m.private_chat_id = ?
          AND m.deleted_for_everyone_at IS NULL
          AND NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM message_hidden h
            WHERE h.message_id = m.id AND h.user_id = ?
          )
        ORDER BY m.created_at DESC, m.id DESC
        LIMIT 300
      ) ORDER BY createdAt ASC, id ASC
    `).bind(chatId, userId).all();

    await db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO private_chat_reads (chat_id, user_id, last_read_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f','now'))
      ON CONFLICT(chat_id, user_id)
      DO UPDATE SET last_read_at = excluded.last_read_at
    `).bind(chatId, userId).run();

    return json({ messages: await attachReactions(db, userId, result.results || []) });
  }

  if (request.method === 'POST') {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    let message = String(body.text || '').trim();

    if (!message || message.length > 4000) {
      return json({ error: 'متن پیام معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
    }

    const typed = await validateTypedBody(db, userId, message);
    if (!typed.ok) return json({ error: 'نوع پیام مجاز نیست.' }, 400);
    message = typed.text;

    const otherId = chat.user_low_id === userId
      ? chat.user_high_id
      : chat.user_low_id;

    if (await blocked(db, userId, otherId)) {
      return json({ error: 'ارسال پیام به این کاربر امکان‌پذیر نیست.' }, 403);
    }

    await db.batch([
      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO messages
          (id, group_id, private_chat_id, sender_id, body, created_at)
        VALUES (?, NULL, ?, ?, ?, strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f','now'))
      `).bind(uid(), chatId, userId, message),

      db.prepare(`
        UPDATE private_chats
        SET last_message_at = datetime('now')
        WHERE id = ?
      `).bind(chatId)
    ]);

    return json({ message: 'پیام ارسال شد.' });
  }

  return json({ error: 'روش درخواست پشتیبانی نمی‌شود.' }, 405);
}

async function sendGift(request, db, userId, chatId) {
  const chat = await getPrivateChat(db, userId, chatId);
  if (!chat) return json({ error: 'گفتگو پیدا نشد.' }, 404);

  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const currency = String(body.currency || '');
  const amount = Number(body.amount);

  if (!['coins', 'stars'].includes(currency)) {
    return json({ error: 'نوع هدیه معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
  }

  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(amount) || amount <= 0 || amount > 1000000000000) {
    return json({ error: 'مقدار هدیه معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
  }

  const recipientId = chat.user_low_id === userId
    ? chat.user_high_id
    : chat.user_low_id;

  if (await blocked(db, userId, recipientId)) {
    return json({ error: 'ارسال هدیه به این کاربر امکان‌پذیر نیست.' }, 403);
  }

  const sender = await publicProfile(db, userId);
  const recipient = await publicProfile(db, recipientId);

  if (!sender || !recipient) {
    return json({ error: 'اطلاعات کاربران پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
  }

  const owner = sender.role === 'owner';
  const giftId = uid();

  const event = {
    type: 'core_gift',
    giftId,
    senderId: userId,
    senderName: sender.displayName || sender.username,
    recipientId,
    recipientName: recipient.displayName || recipient.username,
    currency,
    amount,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  const messageBody = JSON.stringify(event);

  try {
    const statements = [];

    if (currency === 'coins') {
      statements.push(db.prepare(`
        UPDATE users
        SET balance = balance - ?
        WHERE id = ?
          AND (? = 1 OR balance >= ?)
      `).bind(owner ? 0 : amount, userId, owner ? 1 : 0, amount));

      statements.push(db.prepare(`
        UPDATE users
        SET balance = balance + ?
        WHERE id = ?
          AND changes() = 1
      `).bind(amount, recipientId));
    } else {
      statements.push(db.prepare(`
        UPDATE player_profiles
        SET stars = stars - ?
        WHERE user_id = ?
          AND (? = 1 OR stars >= ?)
      `).bind(owner ? 0 : amount, userId, owner ? 1 : 0, amount));

      statements.push(db.prepare(`
        UPDATE player_profiles
        SET stars = stars + ?
        WHERE user_id = ?
          AND changes() = 1
      `).bind(amount, recipientId));
    }

    statements.push(db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO messages
        (id, group_id, private_chat_id, sender_id, body, created_at)
      SELECT ?, NULL, ?, ?, ?, strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f','now')
      WHERE changes() = 1
    `).bind(giftId, chatId, userId, messageBody));

    statements.push(db.prepare(`
      UPDATE private_chats
      SET last_message_at = datetime('now')
      WHERE id = ?
        AND EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM messages
          WHERE id = ?
        )
    `).bind(chatId, giftId));

    const result = await db.batch(statements);

    const inserted = result?.[2]?.meta?.changes || 0;
    if (!inserted) {
      return json({ error: 'موجودی کافی نیست یا ارسال هدیه انجام نشد.' }, 400);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('GIFT_ERROR', error);
    return json({ error: 'ارسال هدیه انجام نشد.' }, 400);
  }

  return json({
    message: 'هدیه با موفقیت ارسال شد.',
    gift: event
  });
}

async function privateList(db, userId) {
  const result = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT
      pc.id AS chatId,
      u.id AS userId,
      u.username,
      u.display_name AS displayName,
      u.avatar,
      CASE WHEN p.background LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(p.background, 5) END AS avatarImage,
      p.role,
      pc.last_message_at AS lastMessageAt,
      (
        SELECT m.body FROM messages m
        WHERE m.private_chat_id = pc.id
        ORDER BY m.created_at DESC, m.id DESC LIMIT 1
      ) AS lastMessage,
      (
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM messages m
        WHERE m.private_chat_id = pc.id
          AND m.sender_id <> ?
          AND m.created_at > COALESCE(
            (
              SELECT r.last_read_at
              FROM private_chat_reads r
              WHERE r.chat_id = pc.id AND r.user_id = ?
            ),
            '1970-01-01 00:00:00'
          )
          AND m.deleted_for_everyone_at IS NULL
          AND NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM message_hidden h
            WHERE h.message_id = m.id AND h.user_id = ?
          )
      ) AS unreadCount
    FROM private_chats pc
    JOIN users u ON u.id = CASE
      WHEN pc.user_low_id = ? THEN pc.user_high_id
      ELSE pc.user_low_id END
    LEFT JOIN player_profiles p ON p.user_id = u.id
    WHERE pc.user_low_id = ? OR pc.user_high_id = ?
    ORDER BY pc.last_message_at DESC
  `).bind(userId, userId, userId, userId, userId, userId).all();

  return result.results || [];
}

async function shopList(db) {
  const result = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT
      id, category, name, description,
      price_amount AS priceAmount,
      price_currency AS priceCurrency,
      asset_key AS assetKey
    FROM shop_items
    WHERE is_active = 1
    ORDER BY category, price_amount
  `).all();

  return result.results || [];
}

async function buyItem(db, userId, itemId) {
  const item = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT * FROM shop_items
    WHERE id = ? AND is_active = 1
  `).bind(itemId).first();

  if (!item) return json({ error: 'محصول پیدا نشد.' }, 404);

  if (!['coins', 'stars'].includes(item.price_currency)) {
    return json({ error: 'واحد پرداخت معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
  }

  const amount = Number(item.price_amount);

  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(amount) || amount <= 0) {
    return json({ error: 'قیمت محصول معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
  }

  const owner = await isOwner(db, userId);

  const inventoryId = uid();
  const transactionId = uid();

  const debit = item.price_currency === 'coins'
    ? db.prepare(`
        UPDATE users
        SET balance = balance - CASE WHEN ? = 1 THEN 0 ELSE ? END
        WHERE id = ?
          AND (? = 1 OR balance >= ?)
      `).bind(owner ? 1 : 0, amount, userId, owner ? 1 : 0, amount)
    : db.prepare(`
        UPDATE player_profiles
        SET stars = stars - CASE WHEN ? = 1 THEN 0 ELSE ? END
        WHERE user_id = ?
          AND (? = 1 OR stars >= ?)
      `).bind(owner ? 1 : 0, amount, userId, owner ? 1 : 0, amount);

  try {
    const result = await db.batch([
      debit,

      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO user_inventory (id, user_id, item_id, equipped)
        SELECT ?, ?, ?, 0
        WHERE changes() = 1
      `).bind(inventoryId, userId, itemId),

      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO shop_transactions
          (id, user_id, item_id, amount, currency, status)
        SELECT ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'completed'
        WHERE EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM user_inventory
          WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?
        )
      `).bind(
        transactionId, userId, itemId, owner ? 0 : amount,
        item.price_currency, inventoryId, userId
      )
    ]);

    if (!result?.[1]?.meta?.changes) {
      return json({ error: 'موجودی کافی نیست یا این محصول قبلاً خریداری شده است.' }, 400);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('SHOP_BUY_ERROR', error);
    return json({ error: 'خرید انجام نشد؛ احتمالاً محصول را قبلاً خریده‌ای.' }, 400);
  }

  return json({
    message: owner
      ? 'خرید رایگان Owner با موفقیت انجام شد.'
      : 'خرید با موفقیت انجام شد.'
  });
}

async function createPaidGroup(request, env) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const userId = await sessionUser(request, db);

  if (!userId) return json({ error: 'ابتدا وارد حساب شو.' }, 401);

  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const name = String(body.name || '').trim();
  const description = String(body.description || '').trim();

  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 50) {
    return json({ error: 'نام گروه باید بین ۲ تا ۵۰ حرف باشد.' }, 400);
  }

  if (description.length > 500) {
    return json({ error: 'توضیحات گروه بیش از حد طولانی است.' }, 400);
  }

  const cost = 5000000000;
  const owner = await isOwner(db, userId);

  const user = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT balance FROM users WHERE id = ?
  `).bind(userId).first();

  if (!user || (!owner && Number(user.balance) < cost)) {
    return json({
      error: 'برای ساخت گروه به ۵ میلیارد کوین نیاز داری.'
    }, 400);
  }

  const groupId = uid();
  const approval = body.joinApproval ? 1 : 0;

  try {
    const result = await db.batch([
      db.prepare(`
        UPDATE users
        SET balance = balance - CASE WHEN ? = 1 THEN 0 ELSE ? END
        WHERE id = ?
          AND (? = 1 OR balance >= ?)
      `).bind(owner ? 1 : 0, cost, userId, owner ? 1 : 0, cost),

      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO groups
          (id, name, description, owner_id, is_official,
           join_approval, invite_enabled)
        SELECT ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, 1
        WHERE changes() = 1
      `).bind(groupId, name, description, userId, approval),

      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO group_members (
          group_id, user_id, role,
          can_invite, can_remove_members, can_delete_messages,
          can_manage_admins, can_edit_group, can_manage_invites,
          can_manage_requests, can_pin_messages
        )
        SELECT ?, ?, 'owner', 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1
        WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM groups WHERE id = ?)
      `).bind(groupId, userId, groupId),

      db.prepare(`
        UPDATE player_profiles
        SET memberships = memberships + 1
        WHERE user_id = ?
          AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM groups WHERE id = ?)
      `).bind(userId, groupId)
    ]);

    if (!result?.[1]?.meta?.changes) {
      return json({ error: 'ساخت گروه انجام نشد.' }, 400);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('PAID_GROUP_ERROR', error);
    return json({ error: 'ساخت گروه انجام نشد.' }, 400);
  }

  return json({
    message: owner
      ? 'گروه رایگان برای Owner ساخته شد.'
      : 'گروه ساخته شد؛ ۵ میلیارد کوین کسر شد.',
    group: {
      id: groupId,
      name,
      description,
      ownerId: userId,
      isOfficial: 0,
      joinApproval: approval,
      myRole: 'owner',
      isMember: 1,
      memberCount: 1
    }
  });
}

async function handleCore(request, env, path) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const userId = await sessionUser(request, db);

  if (!userId) return json({ error: 'ابتدا وارد حساب شو.' }, 401);

  if (path === '/api/core/shop' && request.method === 'GET') {
    return json({
      items: await shopList(db),
      owner: await isOwner(db, userId)
    });
  }

  if (path === '/api/core/shop/buy' && request.method === 'POST') {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    return buyItem(db, userId, String(body.itemId || ''));
  }

  if (path === '/api/core/private' && request.method === 'GET') {
    return json({ chats: await privateList(db, userId) });
  }

  if (path === '/api/core/private' && request.method === 'POST') {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const result = await createPrivateChat(
      db, userId, String(body.targetUserId || '')
    );

    if (result.error) return json({ error: result.error }, 400);
    return json({ chat: result.chat });
  }

  const giftMatch = path.match(
    /^\/api\/core\/private\/([a-f0-9-]+)\/gift$/i
  );

  if (giftMatch && request.method === 'POST') {
    return sendGift(request, db, userId, giftMatch[1]);
  }

  const privateMatch = path.match(
    /^\/api\/core\/private\/([a-f0-9-]+)\/messages$/i
  );

  if (privateMatch) {
    return privateMessages(request, db, userId, privateMatch[1]);
  }

  const profileMatch = path.match(
    /^\/api\/core\/profile\/([a-f0-9-]+)$/i
  );

  if (profileMatch && request.method === 'GET') {
    const profile = await publicProfile(db, profileMatch[1]);
    if (!profile) return json({ error: 'کاربر پیدا نشد.' }, 404);

    if (profile.role === 'owner') {
      profile.balance = '∞';
      profile.stars = '∞';
    }

    return json({ profile });
  }

  if (path === '/api/core/blocks' && request.method === 'POST') {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const targetId = String(body.targetUserId || '');

    if (!targetId || targetId === userId) {
      return json({ error: 'کاربر مقصد معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
    }

    if (!await publicProfile(db, targetId)) {
      return json({ error: 'کاربر پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
    }

    await db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR IGNORE INTO user_blocks (blocker_id, blocked_user_id)
      VALUES (?, ?)
    `).bind(userId, targetId).run();

    return json({ message: 'کاربر مسدود شد.' });
  }

  if (path === '/api/core/blocks' && request.method === 'DELETE') {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));

    await db.prepare(`
      DELETE FROM user_blocks
      WHERE blocker_id = ? AND blocked_user_id = ?
    `).bind(userId, String(body.targetUserId || '')).run();

    return json({ message: 'مسدودسازی برداشته شد.' });
  }

  if (path === '/api/core/reports' && request.method === 'POST') {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const targetId = String(body.targetUserId || '');
    const reason = String(body.reason || 'other');

    const aliases = {
      spam: 'spam',
      insult: 'insult',
      harassment: 'harassment',
      scam: 'scam',
      impersonation: 'impersonation',
      inappropriate: 'inappropriate',
      privacy: 'privacy',
      threat: 'threat',
      child_safety: 'child_safety',
      other: 'other',
      اسپم: 'spam',
      توهین: 'insult',
      مزاحمت: 'harassment',
      کلاهبرداری: 'scam',
      جعل_هویت: 'impersonation',
      محتوای_نامناسب: 'inappropriate',
      نقض_حریم_خصوصی: 'privacy',
      تهدید: 'threat',
      امنیت_کودک: 'child_safety',
      سایر: 'other'
    };

    const normalizedReason = aliases[reason];

    if (!targetId || targetId === userId || !normalizedReason) {
      return json({ error: 'گزارش معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
    }

    if (!await publicProfile(db, targetId)) {
      return json({ error: 'کاربر پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
    }

    await db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO user_reports
        (id, reporter_id, target_user_id, reason, details)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).bind(
      uid(), userId, targetId, normalizedReason,
      String(body.details || '').slice(0, 1000)
    ).run();

    return json({ message: 'گزارش ثبت شد.' });
  }

  if (path === '/api/core/search/users' && request.method === 'GET') {
    const q = (new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('q') || '').trim();

    if (q.length < 2) return json({ users: [] });

    const result = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT
        u.id,
        u.username,
        u.display_name AS displayName,
        u.avatar,
        CASE WHEN p.background LIKE 'img:%' THEN substr(p.background, 5) END AS avatarImage,
        p.role,
        p.level
      FROM users u
      LEFT JOIN player_profiles p ON p.user_id = u.id
      WHERE u.id != ?
        AND (
          u.username LIKE '%' || ? || '%' COLLATE NOCASE
          OR u.display_name LIKE '%' || ? || '%' COLLATE NOCASE
        )
      ORDER BY
        CASE WHEN u.username = ? COLLATE NOCASE THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
        u.display_name
      LIMIT 30
    `).bind(userId, q, q, q).all();

    return json({ users: result.results || [] });
  }

  if (path === '/api/core/search/groups' && request.method === 'GET') {
    const q = (new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('q') || '').trim();

    if (q.length < 2) return json({ groups: [] });

    const result = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT
        g.id,
        g.name,
        g.description,
        g.owner_id AS ownerId,
        g.is_official AS isOfficial,
        g.join_approval AS joinApproval,
        COUNT(DISTINCT gm.user_id) AS memberCount,
        CASE WHEN mine.user_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END AS isMember
      FROM groups g
      LEFT JOIN group_members gm ON gm.group_id = g.id
      LEFT JOIN group_members mine
        ON mine.group_id = g.id AND mine.user_id = ?
      WHERE g.name LIKE '%' || ? || '%' COLLATE NOCASE
      GROUP BY g.id
      ORDER BY memberCount DESC, g.name
      LIMIT 30
    `).bind(userId, q).all();

    return json({ groups: result.results || [] });
  }

  return json({ error: 'مسیر پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
}
return { createPaidGroup, handleCore };
})();

// ┌─ module: roles ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.roles = (() => {
const { getCookie, sha256 } = __M.security;
const { requireDB } = __M.database;

const json = (data, status = 200) => new Response(
  JSON.stringify(data),
  { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8' } }
);

async function sessionUser(request, db) {
  const token = getCookie(request, 'korean_session');
  if (!token) return null;

  const hash = await sha256(token);
  const row = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT user_id FROM sessions
    WHERE token_hash = ?
    AND julianday(expires_at) > julianday('now')
  `).bind(hash).first();

  return row?.user_id || null;
}

async function handleRoles(request, env, path) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const userId = await sessionUser(request, db);

  if (!userId) return json({ error: 'ابتدا وارد حساب شو.' }, 401);

  if (path === '/api/roles/me' && request.method === 'GET') {
    const row = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT role FROM player_profiles WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(userId).first();

    return json({ role: row?.role || 'player' });
  }

  if (path === '/api/admin/roles' && request.method === 'POST') {
    const actor = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT role FROM player_profiles WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(userId).first();

    if (actor?.role !== 'owner') {
      return json({ error: 'فقط Owner اجازه مدیریت نقش‌ها را دارد.' }, 403);
    }

    const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    const targetId = String(body?.userId || '');
    const role = String(body?.role || '');

    if (!targetId || !['admin', 'support', 'player'].includes(role)) {
      return json({ error: 'اطلاعات نقش معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
    }

    const target = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT role FROM player_profiles WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(targetId).first();

    if (!target) return json({ error: 'کاربر پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
    if (target.role === 'owner') {
      return json({ error: 'تغییر نقش Owner از این مسیر مجاز نیست.' }, 403);
    }

    await db.prepare(`
      UPDATE player_profiles SET role = ?, updated_at = datetime('now')
      WHERE user_id = ?
    `).bind(role, targetId).run();

    return json({ message: 'نقش کاربر با موفقیت تغییر کرد.', role });
  }

  return json({ error: 'مسیر نقش‌ها پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
}
return { handleRoles };
})();

// ┌─ module: admin ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.admin = (() => {
const { getCookie, sha256 } = __M.security;
const { requireDB } = __M.database;

const json = (data, status = 200) => new Response(
  JSON.stringify(data),
  { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8' } }
);

async function getUserId(request, db) {
  const token = getCookie(request, 'korean_session');
  if (!token) return null;

  const hash = await sha256(token);
  const row = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT user_id
    FROM sessions
    WHERE token_hash = ?
      AND julianday(expires_at) > julianday('now')
  `).bind(hash).first();

  return row?.user_id || null;
}

async function getRole(db, userId) {
  const row = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT role FROM player_profiles WHERE user_id = ?
  `).bind(userId).first();

  return row?.role || 'player';
}

async function handleAdmin(request, env, path) {
  const db = requireDB(env);
  const userId = await getUserId(request, db);

  if (!userId) {
    return json({ error: 'ابتدا وارد حساب شو.' }, 401);
  }

  const role = await getRole(db, userId);

  if (path === '/api/admin/users' && request.method === 'GET') {
    if (role !== 'owner') {
      return json({ error: 'فقط Owner اجازه مدیریت کاربران را دارد.' }, 403);
    }

    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const query = (url.searchParams.get('q') || '').trim().slice(0, 60);

    if (query.length < 2) {
      return json({ users: [] });
    }

    const pattern = `%${query}%`;
    const result = await db.prepare(`
      SELECT
        u.id,
        u.username,
        u.display_name AS displayName,
        u.avatar,
        COALESCE(p.role, 'player') AS role,
        u.created_at AS createdAt
      FROM users u
      LEFT JOIN player_profiles p ON p.user_id = u.id
      WHERE u.username LIKE ? OR u.display_name LIKE ?
      ORDER BY u.created_at DESC
      LIMIT 30
    `).bind(pattern, pattern).all();

    return json({ users: result.results || [] });
  }

  if (path === '/api/admin/reports' && request.method === 'GET') {
    if (!['admin', 'owner'].includes(role)) {
      return json({ error: 'دسترسی به گزارش‌ها مجاز نیست.' }, 403);
    }

    const [users, messages] = await Promise.all([
      db.prepare(`
        SELECT * FROM user_reports
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
        LIMIT 100
      `).all(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT * FROM message_reports
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
        LIMIT 100
      `).all()
    ]);

    return json({
      userReports: users.results || [],
      messageReports: messages.results || []
    });
  }

  const userReportMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/admin\/reports\/user\/([^/]+)$/);
  const messageReportMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/admin\/reports\/message\/([^/]+)$/);

  if (userReportMatch && request.method === 'PATCH') {
    if (!['admin', 'owner'].includes(role)) {
      return json({ error: 'دسترسی به گزارش‌ها مجاز نیست.' }, 403);
    }

    const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    const status = String(body?.status || '');

    if (!['pending', 'reviewed', 'dismissed'].includes(status)) {
      return json({ error: 'وضعیت گزارش معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
    }

    const result = await db.prepare(`
      UPDATE user_reports
      SET status = ?
      WHERE id = ?
    `).bind(status, userReportMatch[1]).run();

    if (!result.meta?.changes) {
      return json({ error: 'گزارش پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
    }

    return json({ message: 'وضعیت گزارش به‌روزرسانی شد.', status });
  }

  if (messageReportMatch && request.method === 'PATCH') {
    if (!['admin', 'owner'].includes(role)) {
      return json({ error: 'دسترسی به گزارش‌ها مجاز نیست.' }, 403);
    }

    const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    const status = String(body?.status || '');
    const action = String(body?.action || '').slice(0, 500);

    if (!['pending', 'reviewed', 'action_taken', 'dismissed'].includes(status)) {
      return json({ error: 'وضعیت گزارش معتبر نیست.' }, 400);
    }

    const result = await db.prepare(`
      UPDATE message_reports
      SET status = ?,
          reviewed_by = ?,
          action = ?,
          reviewed_at = datetime('now')
      WHERE id = ?
    `).bind(status, userId, action, messageReportMatch[1]).run();

    if (!result.meta?.changes) {
      return json({ error: 'گزارش پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
    }

    return json({ message: 'گزارش پیام به‌روزرسانی شد.', status });
  }

  return json({ error: 'مسیر پنل مدیریت پیدا نشد.' }, 404);
}
return { handleAdmin };
})();

// ┌─ module: index ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
__M.index = (() => {
const {
  register, login, me, logout, updateProfile
} = __M.auth;

const {sameOrigin} = __M.security;
const {handleSocial} = __M.social;
const {handleCore, createPaidGroup} = __M.core;
const {handleRoles} = __M.roles;
const {handleAdmin} = __M.admin;
const {handleExtras, isExtrasPath} = __M.extras;
const {ensureSchema} = __M.bootstrap;

const response = (data, status = 200) => new Response(
  JSON.stringify(data),
  {status, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json; charset=utf-8'}}
);

return {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const path = url.pathname;

    if (path.startsWith('/api/')) {
      if (request.method === 'OPTIONS')
        return new Response(null, {status:204});

      if (['POST','PUT','PATCH','DELETE'].includes(request.method)
          && !sameOrigin(request)) {
        return response({error:'درخواست نامعتبر است.'}, 403);
      }

      try {
        await ensureSchema(env);

        if (path.startsWith('/api/roles/') || path.startsWith('/api/admin/roles')) {
          return await handleRoles(request, env, path);
        }
        if (path.startsWith('/api/admin/')) {
          return await handleAdmin(request, env, path);
        }

        if (path === '/api/health' && request.method === 'GET')
          return response({status:'online', project:'KOREAN CORE'});

        if (path === '/api/register' && request.method === 'POST')
          return await register(request, env);

        if (path === '/api/login' && request.method === 'POST')
          return await login(request, env);

        if (path === '/api/me' && request.method === 'GET')
          return await me(request, env);

        if (path === '/api/profile' && request.method === 'PATCH')
          return await updateProfile(request, env);

        if (path === '/api/logout' && request.method === 'POST')
          return await logout(request, env);

        if (isExtrasPath(path))
          return await handleExtras(request, env, path);

        if (path.startsWith('/api/core/'))
          return await handleCore(request, env, path);

        if (path === '/api/groups' && request.method === 'POST')
          return await createPaidGroup(request, env);

        if (path === '/api/groups' || path.startsWith('/api/groups/'))
          return await handleSocial(request, env, path);

        return response({error:'مسیر API پیدا نشد.'}, 404);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('API_ERROR:', path, error);
        const message = error?.message === 'DATABASE_NOT_CONFIGURED'
          ? 'اتصال دیتابیس هنوز تنظیم نشده است.'
          : 'خطای داخلی سرور.';
        return response({error:message}, 500);
      }
    }

    if (env.ASSETS) return env.ASSETS.fetch(request);
    return new Response('KOREAN CORE', {status:200});
  }
};
})();

const __api = __M.index;
