// ── /upload.js (10.0 KB)
ASSETS["/upload.js"] = { type: "application/javascript; charset=utf-8", etag: '5147e0576c61', text: `(() => {
  'use strict';
  const ID = /^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i;
  const notify = t => {
    const el = document.getElementById('toast'); if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = t; el.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(window.kcMediaToast); window.kcMediaToast = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('show'), 2600);
  };

  const blobToBase64 = blob => new Promise((res, rej) => {
    const r = new FileReader();
    r.onload = () => res(String(r.result).split(',')[1] || '');
    r.onerror = () => rej(new Error('خواندن فایل ناموفق بود.'));
    r.readAsDataURL(blob);
  });

  function loadImage(file) {
    return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(file), img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); res(img); };
      img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); rej(new Error('فایل انتخاب‌شده تصویر معتبر نیست.')); };
      img.src = url;
    });
  }

  // Resize/compress in the browser so uploads stay small and fast.
  async function prepare(file, { maxDim = 1280, square = false } = {}) {
    if (!file || !String(file.type).startsWith('image/')) throw new Error('فقط فایل تصویر قابل انتخاب است.');
    if (file.type === 'image/gif' && !square && file.size <= 600000)
      return { mime: 'image/gif', data: await blobToBase64(file) };

    const img = await loadImage(file);
    let sw = img.naturalWidth, sh = img.naturalHeight, sx = 0, sy = 0;
    if (square) { const s = Math.min(sw, sh); sx = (sw - s) / 2; sy = (sh - s) / 2; sw = sh = s; }
    const k = Math.min(1, maxDim / Math.max(sw, sh));
    const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(sw * k)), h = Math.max(1, Math.round(sh * k));
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, sw, sh, 0, 0, w, h);

    let blob = null;
    for (const q of [0.86, 0.74, 0.62, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3]) {
      blob = await new Promise(r => canvas.toBlob(r, 'image/jpeg', q));
      if (blob && blob.size <= 620000) break;
    }
    if (!blob || blob.size > 620000) throw new Error('حجم تصویر بعد از فشرده‌سازی هم زیاد است.');
    return { mime: 'image/jpeg', data: await blobToBase64(blob) };
  }

  async function upload(file, opts) {
    const payload = await prepare(file, opts);
    const r = await fetch('/api/upload', {
      method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.error || 'آپلود انجام نشد.');
    return d.id;
  }

  async function uploadAudio(blob, mime) {
    const data = await blobToBase64(blob);
    if (data.length > 900000) throw new Error('پیام صوتی خیلی طولانی است.');
    const r = await fetch('/api/upload', {
      method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ mime, data })
    });
    const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.error || 'آپلود صدا انجام نشد.');
    return d.id;
  }

  function pick() {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const input = document.createElement('input');
      input.type = 'file'; input.accept = 'image/*';
      input.addEventListener('change', () => resolve(input.files?.[0] || null));
      input.addEventListener('cancel', () => resolve(null));
      input.click();
    });
  }

  const imgUrl = id => ID.test(String(id || '')) ? '/api/img/' + id : '';

  function lightbox(src) {
    const wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'kc-lightbox';
    const img = document.createElement('img'); img.src = src; img.alt = '';
    const close = document.createElement('button'); close.type = 'button'; close.textContent = '×';
    wrap.append(img, close);
    wrap.addEventListener('click', () => wrap.remove());
    document.body.appendChild(wrap);
  }

  function parse(body) {
    const t = String(body || '').trimStart();
    if (!t.startsWith('{')) return null;
    try { const o = JSON.parse(t); return o && typeof o === 'object' ? o : null; } catch { return null; }
  }

  function preview(body) {
    const o = parse(body);
    if (o?.type === 'kc_image') return '📷 ' + (o.caption ? String(o.caption) : 'تصویر');
    if (o?.type === 'kc_voice') return '🎤 پیام صوتی';
    if (o?.type === 'core_gift') return '🎁 هدیه';
    return String(body || '');
  }

  function linkify(el, text) {
    String(text).split(/(https?:\\/\\/[^\\s<>"']+)/g).forEach((part, i) => {
      if (i % 2) {
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = part; a.textContent = part; a.target = '_blank'; a.rel = 'noopener noreferrer nofollow';
        a.className = 'kc-link'; el.appendChild(a);
      } else if (part) el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(part));
    });
  }

  const audioCache = new Map();
  const fmt = n => { n = Math.max(0, Math.round(n || 0)); return Math.floor(n / 60) + ':' + String(n % 60).padStart(2, '0'); };

  function voicePlayer(o) {
    const wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'kc-voice'; wrap.dir = 'ltr';
    const btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.type = 'button'; btn.textContent = '▶';
    const track = document.createElement('div'); track.className = 'kc-voice-track';
    const bar = document.createElement('i'); track.appendChild(bar);
    const time = document.createElement('span'); time.textContent = fmt(o.dur);
    wrap.append(btn, track, time);

    let audio = audioCache.get(o.id);
    if (!audio) { audio = new Audio(imgUrl(o.id)); audio.preload = 'metadata'; audioCache.set(o.id, audio); }
    const total = () => (isFinite(audio.duration) && audio.duration > 0 ? audio.duration : o.dur || 0);
    const paint = () => {
      bar.style.width = (total() ? Math.min(100, audio.currentTime / total() * 100) : 0) + '%';
      btn.textContent = audio.paused ? '▶' : '⏸';
      time.textContent = fmt(audio.paused && audio.currentTime === 0 ? total() : audio.currentTime);
    };
    audio.ontimeupdate = paint; audio.onplay = paint; audio.onpause = paint;
    audio.onended = () => { audio.currentTime = 0; paint(); };
    audio.onerror = () => notify('پخش پیام صوتی ممکن نشد.');
    paint();

    btn.addEventListener('click', e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (!audio.paused) return audio.pause();
      if (window.KC_AUDIO && window.KC_AUDIO !== audio) window.KC_AUDIO.pause();
      window.KC_AUDIO = audio;
      audio.play().catch(() => notify('پخش صدا ممکن نشد.'));
    });
    track.addEventListener('click', e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const rect = track.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (total()) audio.currentTime = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width)) * total();
      paint();
    });
    return wrap;
  }

  const JUMBO = /^(?:\\p{Extended_Pictographic}[\\u200d\\ufe0f\\u{1F3FB}-\\u{1F3FF}]*\\s*){1,5}$/u;

  function renderBody(el, body) {
    const o = parse(body);
    if (o?.type === 'kc_voice' && imgUrl(o.id)) { el.appendChild(voicePlayer(o)); return; }
    if (!o && JUMBO.test(String(body).trim())) el.classList.add('kc-jumbo');
    if (o?.type === 'kc_image' && imgUrl(o.id)) {
      const img = document.createElement('img');
      img.className = 'kc-chat-img'; img.loading = 'lazy'; img.alt = 'تصویر'; img.src = imgUrl(o.id);
      img.addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); lightbox(img.src); });
      el.appendChild(img);
      if (o.caption) { const c = document.createElement('div'); c.className = 'kc-caption'; linkify(c, o.caption); el.appendChild(c); }
      return;
    }
    linkify(el, body);
  }

  async function react(messageId, emoji) {
    try {
      const r = await fetch('/api/core/reactions', {
        method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ messageId, emoji })
      });
      const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.error || 'ثبت ری‌اکشن ناموفق بود.');
      document.dispatchEvent(new Event('kc:reload'));
    } catch (e) { notify(e.message); }
  }

  function reactionsRow(message) {
    if (!message.reactions?.length) return null;
    const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'kc-reactions';
    message.reactions.forEach(r => {
      const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button';
      b.className = 'kc-react-chip' + (r.mine ? ' mine' : '');
      b.textContent = r.emoji + ' ' + Number(r.count).toLocaleString('fa-IR');
      b.addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); react(message.id, r.emoji); });
      row.appendChild(b);
    });
    return row;
  }

  const FX = {
    confetti(n = 42) {
      const box = document.createElement('div'); box.className = 'kc-confetti';
      const colors = ['#6ea8ff', '#fbbf24', '#fb7185', '#34d399', '#a78bfa', '#f472b6'];
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const p = document.createElement('i');
        p.style.cssText = \`left:\${Math.random() * 100}%;background:\${colors[i % colors.length]};\` +
          \`--dx:\${(Math.random() - .5) * 240}px;--rot:\${Math.random() * 720}deg;animation-delay:\${Math.random() * .25}s;animation-duration:\${1.3 + Math.random() * .9}s\`;
        box.appendChild(p);
      }
      document.body.appendChild(box); setTimeout(() => box.remove(), 2600);
      navigator.vibrate?.(30);
    },
    countTo(el, to, ms = 700) {
      if (!el) return;
      const from = Number(el.dataset.v ?? to); el.dataset.v = to;
      if (from === to || matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) { el.textContent = to.toLocaleString('fa-IR'); return; }
      const t0 = performance.now();
      const step = t => {
        const k = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / ms), e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3);
        el.textContent = Math.round(from + (to - from) * e).toLocaleString('fa-IR');
        if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
      };
      requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }
  };
  window.KC_FX = FX;

  window.KC_MEDIA = { upload, uploadAudio, pick, lightbox, parse, preview, renderBody, reactionsRow, react, imgUrl, notify };
})();
` };

function toast(message) {

  window.toastTimer = setTimeout(() => box.classList.remove('show'), 2800);

function avatarMarkup(number, image) {
  if (image && /^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i.test(String(image)))
    return \`<img class="avatar-photo" alt="" src="/api/img/\${image}">\`;
  number = Math.max(0, Math.min(47, Number(number) || 0));
  const categories = Object.keys(window.AVATAR_PALETTES);
  const categoryIndex = Math.floor(number / 8);
  const paletteIndex = number % 8;
  const palette = window.AVATAR_PALETTES[categories[categoryIndex]]?.[paletteIndex]
    || window.AVATAR_PALETTES['پیشفرض'][0];

  return \`<div class="person avatar-look-\${number % 8}"
    style="--hair:\${palette[0]};--skin:\${palette[1]};--shirt:\${palette[2]}">
    <div class="avatar-shadow"></div>
    <div class="body"></div>
    <div class="neck"></div>
    <div class="face">
      <span class="brow brow-left"></span>
      <span class="brow brow-right"></span>
      <span class="eye eye-left"></span>
      <span class="eye eye-right"></span>
      <span class="nose"></span>
      <span class="mouth"></span>
    </div>
    <div class="hair"></div>
    <div class="ear ear-left"></div>
    <div class="ear ear-right"></div>
    <div class="avatar-detail"></div>
  </div>\`;
}

function drawAvatars(category) {
  currentCategory = category;
  const grid = $('avatarGrid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';

  const categories = Object.keys(window.AVATAR_PALETTES);

  window.AVATAR_PALETTES[category].forEach((palette, index) => {
    const number = categories.indexOf(category) * 8 + index;
    const item = document.createElement('button');
    item.type = 'button';
    item.className = 'avatar' + (selectedAvatar === number ? ' selected' : '');
    item.innerHTML = avatarMarkup(number);
    item.addEventListener('click', () => {
      selectedAvatar = number;
      drawAvatars(category);
    });
    grid.appendChild(item);
  });
}



function setMode(next) {
  mode = next;
  $('app').classList.toggle('register-mode', mode === 'register');
  $('loginTab').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'login');
  $('registerTab').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'register');
  $('avatarSection').classList.toggle('show', mode === 'register');

  $('formTitle').textContent = mode === 'login' ? 'خوش برگشتی!' : 'هویتت را بساز';
  $('formDesc').textContent = mode === 'login'
    ? 'اطلاعات حسابت را وارد کن.'
    : 'نام بازیکن، مشخصات ورود و آواتارت را انتخاب کن.';

  $('submitBtn').innerHTML = mode === 'login'
    ? 'ورود به بازی <span>←</span>'
    : 'ساخت حساب و ورود <span>←</span>';

  $('password').autocomplete = mode === 'login' ? 'current-password' : 'new-password';
}

async function request(path, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(path, {
    credentials:'same-origin',
    ...options,
    headers:{
      ...(options.body ? {'Content-Type':'application/json'} : {}),
      ...(options.headers || {})
    }
  });

  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

  if (!response.ok)
    throw new Error(result.error || 'ارتباط با سرور برقرار نشد.');

  return result;
}

function showAuth() {
  authView.classList.remove('hidden');
  profileView.classList.add('hidden');
}

function showProfile(user) {
  if (!user) return showAuth();

  currentUser = user;
  window.KC_USER = user;
  document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kc:user', {detail:user}));
  authView.classList.add('hidden');
  profileView.classList.remove('hidden');

  $('profileName').textContent = user.displayName || user.username;
  $('profileUsername').textContent = '@' + user.username;
  $('profileBio').textContent = user.bio || 'هنوز بیوگرافی ثبت نشده است.';
  $('profileAvatar').innerHTML = avatarMarkup(Number(user.avatar) || 0, user.avatarImage);

  const banner = document.querySelector('.profile-banner');
  if (banner) {
    const ok = user.bannerImage && /^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i.test(user.bannerImage);
    banner.classList.toggle('has-img', !!ok);
    if (ok) banner.style.setProperty('--banner-img', \`url("/api/img/\${user.bannerImage}")\`);
    else banner.style.removeProperty('--banner-img');
  }
  loadDaily();

  const roles = {
    player:'PLAYER',
    owner:'OWNER',
    admin:'ADMIN',
    support:'SUPPORT',
    premium:'PREMIUM',
    creator:'CREATOR'
  };

  $('profileRole').textContent = roles[user.role] || 'PLAYER';

  const level = Math.max(1, Number(user.level) || 1);
  const xp = Math.max(0, Number(user.xp) || 0);
  const xpNext = Math.max(1, Number(user.xpNext) || 100);
  const percent = Math.min(100, Math.round((xp / xpNext) * 100));

  $('levelValue').textContent = level.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
  $('xpValue').textContent = xp.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
  $('xpNext').textContent = xpNext.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
  $('xpFill').style.width = percent + '%';
  $('xpPercent').textContent = percent.toLocaleString('fa-IR') + '٪';

  window.KC_FX.countTo($('coinValue'), Number(user.balance || 0));
  window.KC_FX.countTo($('starValue'), Number(user.stars || 0));

  if (window.KC_LAST_LEVEL && level > window.KC_LAST_LEVEL) {
    window.KC_FX.confetti(70);
    toast(\`🎉 تبریک! به سطح \${level.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} رسیدی!\`);
  }
  window.KC_LAST_LEVEL = level;
  renderAchievements(user);

  $('messagesValue').textContent = Number(user.messages || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR');
  $('reactionsValue').textContent = Number(user.reactions || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR');
  $('voiceValue').textContent = Number(user.voiceMessages || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR');
  $('membershipsValue').textContent = Number(user.memberships || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR');
  $('warningsValue').textContent = Number(user.warnings || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR');

  $('editName').value = user.displayName || '';
  $('editBio').value = user.bio || '';
  $('bioCount').textContent = (user.bio || '').length.toLocaleString('fa-IR');


}

$('authForm').addEventListener('submit', async event => {
  event.preventDefault();

  const button = $('submitBtn');
  const username = $('username').value.trim();
  const password = $('password').value;

  if (mode === 'register' && password.length < 8) {
    toast('رمز عبور باید حداقل ۸ کاراکتر باشد.');
    return;
  }

  if (mode === 'register' && !/^[A-Za-z0-9_]{7,24}$/.test(username)) {
    toast('نام کاربری باید ۷ تا ۲۴ حرف انگلیسی، عدد یا _ باشد.');
    return;
  }

  button.disabled = true;
  button.textContent = 'در حال اتصال به سرور...';

  try {
    const result = await request(
      mode === 'login' ? '/api/login' : '/api/register',
      {
        method:'POST',
        body:JSON.stringify({
          username,
          password,
          displayName:$('gameName').value.trim(),
          avatar:selectedAvatar
        })
      }
    );

    if (result.user) {
      $('authForm').reset();
      showProfile(result.user);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    toast(error.message);
  } finally {
    button.disabled = false;
    button.innerHTML = mode === 'login'
      ? 'ورود به بازی <span>←</span>'
      : 'ساخت حساب و ورود <span>←</span>';
  }
});

$('editBio').addEventListener('input', () => {
  $('bioCount').textContent = $('editBio').value.length.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
});

$('profileForm').addEventListener('submit', async event => {
  event.preventDefault();

  const button = $('saveProfileBtn');
  button.disabled = true;
  button.textContent = 'در حال ذخیره...';

  try {
    const result = await request('/api/profile', {
      method:'PATCH',
      body:JSON.stringify({
        displayName:$('editName').value.trim(),
        bio:$('editBio').value.trim(),
        avatar:Number(currentUser?.avatar) || 0
      })
    });

    if (result.user) {
      showProfile(result.user);
      toast('پروفایل ذخیره شد!');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    toast(error.message);
  } finally {
    button.disabled = false;
    button.innerHTML = 'ذخیره تغییرات <span>✓</span>';
  }
});




function closeKoreanModal(id) {
  const modal = $(id);
  if (modal) modal.classList.remove('open');
}

function createKoreanModals() {
  if (!$('avatarModal')) {
    document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', \`
      <div class="kc-modal" id="avatarModal">
        <div class="kc-modal-box avatar-modal-box">
          <button class="kc-modal-close" id="closeAvatarModal">×</button>
          <div class="kc-modal-icon">✦</div>
          <span class="eyebrow">YOUR IDENTITY</span>
          <h2>انتخاب آواتار</h2>
          <p>شخصیت دلخواهت رو انتخاب کن.</p>
          <div class="kc-avatar-grid" id="profileAvatarGrid"></div>
          <div class="kc-upload-row">
            <button type="button" class="kc-cancel" id="uploadAvatarPhoto">📷 آپلود عکس خودت</button>
            <button type="button" class="kc-cancel" id="removeAvatarPhoto">🗑 حذف عکس</button>
          </div>
          <button class="primary kc-confirm" id="confirmAvatar">ذخیره آواتار</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    \`);
  }

  if (!$('logoutModal')) {
    document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', \`
      <div class="kc-modal" id="logoutModal">
        <div class="kc-modal-box logout-modal-box">
          <button class="kc-modal-close" id="closeLogoutModal">×</button>
          <div class="kc-modal-icon logout-icon">↪</div>
          <span class="eyebrow">KOREAN CORE</span>
          <h2>خروج از حساب؟</h2>
          <p>مطمئنی می‌خوای از حساب کاربریت خارج بشی؟</p>
          <div class="kc-modal-actions">
            <button class="kc-cancel" id="cancelLogout">انصراف</button>
            <button class="kc-danger" id="confirmLogout">خروج از حساب</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    \`);
  }
}

let pendingAvatar = 0;

function openAvatarModal() {
  if (!currentUser) return;

  pendingAvatar = Number(currentUser.avatar) || 0;
  const grid = $('profileAvatarGrid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';

  for (let number = 0; number < 48; number++) {
    const item = document.createElement('button');
    item.type = 'button';
    item.className = 'kc-avatar-item' +
      (pendingAvatar === number ? ' selected' : '');
    item.setAttribute('aria-label', 'آواتار ' + (number + 1));
    item.innerHTML = avatarMarkup(number);

    item.addEventListener('click', () => {
      pendingAvatar = number;
      grid.querySelectorAll('.kc-avatar-item').forEach((el, index) => {
        el.classList.toggle('selected', index === number);
      });
    });

    grid.appendChild(item);
  }

  $('avatarModal').classList.add('open');
}

async function saveProfileAvatar() {
  if (!currentUser) return;

  const button = $('confirmAvatar');
  button.disabled = true;
  button.textContent = 'در حال ذخیره...';

  try {
    if (currentUser.avatarImage) {
      await request('/api/profile/image', {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({kind:'avatar', id:null})});
    }
    const result = await request('/api/profile', {
      method: 'PATCH',
      body: JSON.stringify({
        displayName: currentUser.displayName || currentUser.username,
        bio: currentUser.bio || '',
        avatar: pendingAvatar
      })
    });

    if (result.user) {
      showProfile(result.user);
      closeKoreanModal('avatarModal');
      toast('آواتار جدید ذخیره شد!');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    toast(error.message);
  } finally {
    button.disabled = false;
    button.textContent = 'ذخیره آواتار';
  }
}

async function performLogout() {
  const button = $('confirmLogout');
  button.disabled = true;
  button.textContent = 'در حال خروج...';

  try {
    await request('/api/logout', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({})
    });

    currentUser = null;
    window.KC_USER = null;
    document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kc:logout'));
    closeKoreanModal('logoutModal');
    showAuth();
    setMode('login');
    toast('با موفقیت خارج شدی.');
  } catch (error) {
    toast(error.message);
  } finally {
    button.disabled = false;
    button.textContent = 'خروج از حساب';
  }
}

createKoreanModals();

async function setProfileImage(kind, file) {
  try {
    toast('در حال آپلود تصویر...');
    const id = await window.KC_MEDIA.upload(file, kind === 'avatar'
      ? {maxDim: 512, square: true} : {maxDim: 1400});
    const result = await request('/api/profile/image', {
      method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({kind, id})
    });
    if (result.user) showProfile(result.user);
    toast(kind === 'avatar' ? 'عکس پروفایل ذخیره شد!' : 'بنر ذخیره شد!');
    return true;
  } catch (error) { toast(error.message); return false; }
}

$('uploadAvatarPhoto').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const file = await window.KC_MEDIA.pick();
  if (file && await setProfileImage('avatar', file)) closeKoreanModal('avatarModal');
});

$('removeAvatarPhoto').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    const r = await request('/api/profile/image', {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({kind:'avatar', id:null})});
    if (r.user) showProfile(r.user);
    closeKoreanModal('avatarModal');
    toast('عکس حذف شد.');
  } catch (e) { toast(e.message); }
});

$('bannerEdit').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const file = await window.KC_MEDIA.pick();
  if (file) await setProfileImage('banner', file);
});

$('bannerReset').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    const r = await request('/api/profile/image', {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({kind:'banner', id:null})});
    if (r.user) showProfile(r.user);
  } catch (e) { toast(e.message); }
});

async function loadDaily() {
  const card = $('dailyCard');
  if (!card) return;
  try {
    const d = await request('/api/core/daily');
    card.classList.remove('hidden');
    $('dailyBtn').disabled = !d.available;
    $('dailyBtn').textContent = d.available ? 'دریافت جایزه 🎁' : 'امروز گرفتی ✓';
    $('dailyText').textContent = d.available
      ? (d.streak ? \`\${d.streak.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} روز پشت‌سرهم! امروز هم بگیر.\` : 'هر روز بیا و جایزه‌ی بزرگ‌تر بگیر.')
      : \`\${d.streak.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} روز پشت‌سرهم؛ فردا دوباره بیا!\`;
  } catch { card.classList.add('hidden'); }
}

$('dailyBtn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const btn = $('dailyBtn'); btn.disabled = true;
  try {
    const r = await request('/api/core/daily', {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({})});
    toast(r.message);
    window.KC_FX.confetti();
    window.KC_refreshUser();
  } catch (e) { toast(e.message); loadDaily(); }
});

async function startApp() {
  drawAvatars('پیشفرض');
  showAuth();

  try {
    const result = await request('/api/me');
    if (result.user) showProfile(result.user);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
  }
}

const ACHIEVEMENTS = [
  ['💬', 'اولین پیام', 'messages', 1], ['🗣', 'پرحرف', 'messages', 100], ['📢', 'صدای گروه', 'messages', 1000],
  ['⭐', 'ستاره‌باز', 'stars', 10], ['🌟', 'ستاره‌ساز', 'stars', 100],
  ['⚡', 'سطح ۵', 'level', 5], ['🚀', 'سطح ۲۰', 'level', 20],
  ['💰', 'میلیاردر!', 'balance', 10000000], ['❤️', 'محبوب', 'reactions', 25],
  ['🎤', 'صداخوان', 'voiceMessages', 5], ['👥', 'باشگاهی', 'memberships', 3]
];

function renderAchievements(user) {
  const grid = $('achGrid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.replaceChildren();
  let unlocked = 0;
  ACHIEVEMENTS.forEach(([icon, title, key, target]) => {
    const value = Number(user[key] || 0);
    const done = value >= target;
    if (done) unlocked++;
    const tile = document.createElement('div');
    tile.className = 'ach-tile' + (done ? ' done' : '');
    const i = document.createElement('span'); i.className = 'ach-icon'; i.textContent = icon;
    const t = document.createElement('b'); t.textContent = title;
    const bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.className = 'ach-bar';
    const fill = document.createElement('i'); fill.style.width = Math.min(100, value / target * 100) + '%';
    bar.appendChild(fill);
    const sm = document.createElement('small');
    sm.textContent = done ? 'باز شد ✓' : \`\${Math.min(value, target).toLocaleString('fa-IR')} / \${target.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}\`;
    tile.append(i, t, bar, sm);
    grid.appendChild(tile);
  });
  $('achCount').textContent = \`\${unlocked.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} / \${ACHIEVEMENTS.length.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}\`;
}

if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
  window.addEventListener('load', () => navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(() => {}));
}

window.KC_refreshUser = async () => {
  try { const r = await request('/api/me'); if (r.user) showProfile(r.user); } catch {}
};

startApp();
` };

  async function loadGroups(silent = false) {
    if (groupsLoading) return;
    groupsLoading = true;

    const list = $('kcGroupList');
    if (!silent) {
      list.innerHTML = '<div class="kc-social-empty">در حال دریافت گروه‌ها...</div>';
    }

    try {
      const data = await api('/api/groups');

      if (
        $('kcSocialPanel').classList.contains('kc-social-hidden') ||
        !$('kcGroupView').classList.contains('kc-social-hidden')
      ) return;

      groups = Array.isArray(data.groups) ? data.groups : [];

      $('kcGroupTotal').textContent =
        groups.length.toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' گروه';

      list.replaceChildren();

      if (!groups.length) {

        info.className = 'kc-group-info';

        name.className = 'kc-group-name';

          Number(group.memberCount || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' عضو';

          badge.textContent = unread > 99 ? '۹۹+' : unread.toLocaleString('fa-IR');
          item.appendChild(badge);
        }

        item.addEventListener('click', () => openGroup(group.id));
        list.appendChild(item);
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (!silent) {
        list.innerHTML = '';
        const message = document.createElement('div');
        message.className = 'kc-social-empty';
        message.textContent = error.message;
        list.appendChild(message);
      }
    } finally {
      groupsLoading = false;
    }
  }

  function startGroupsRefresh() {
    clearInterval(groupsRefreshTimer);
    groupsRefreshTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (
        document.visibilityState === 'visible' &&
        !$('kcSocialPanel').classList.contains('kc-social-hidden') &&
        $('kcGroupView').classList.contains('kc-social-hidden')
      ) {
        loadGroups(true);
      }

  }

  async function createGroup(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const button = $('kcCreateForm').querySelector('button[type=submit]');
    button.disabled = true;

    try {
      const result = await api('/api/groups', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: $('kcGroupName').value.trim(),
          description: $('kcGroupDescription').value.trim(),
          joinApproval: $('kcGroupApproval').checked
        })
      });

      $('kcCreateForm').reset();
      $('kcCreateForm').classList.add('kc-social-hidden');

      notify(result.message || 'گروه ساخته شد.');

      await loadGroups();

      if (result.group?.id) {
        await openGroup(result.group.id);
      }

    } catch (error) {
      notify(error.message);
    } finally {
      button.disabled = false;
    }
  }

  async function openGroup(id) {
    clearInterval(groupsRefreshTimer);
    groupsRefreshTimer = null;
    try {
      const data = await api('/api/groups/' + encodeURIComponent(id));
      currentGroup = data.group;

      if (!currentGroup) {
        throw new Error('گروه پیدا نشد.');
      }

      $('kcSocialHome').classList.add('kc-social-hidden');
      $('kcGroupView').classList.remove('kc-social-hidden');

      $('kcGroupInfo').textContent =
        currentGroup.description || 'بدون توضیحات';

      $('kcChatName').textContent = currentGroup.name;
      $('kcChatDescription').textContent =
        Number(currentGroup.memberCount || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' عضو';

      const member = Number(currentGroup.isMember) === 1;

      $('kcJoinArea').classList.toggle('kc-social-hidden', member);
      $('kcChat').classList.toggle('kc-social-hidden', !member);

      $('kcLeaveGroup').classList.toggle(
        'kc-social-hidden',
        !member || currentGroup.myRole === 'owner'
      );

      clearInterval(refreshTimer);
      refreshTimer = null;

      if (member) {
        await loadMessages();

        const activeGroupId = currentGroup.id;
        refreshTimer = setInterval(() => {
          if (
            document.visibilityState === "visible" &&
            currentGroup?.id === activeGroupId &&
            !$("kcGroupView").classList.contains("kc-social-hidden")
          ) {
            loadMessages(true);
          }
        }, 1500);
      }

    } catch (error) {
      notify(error.message);
    }
  }

  async function joinCurrentGroup() {
    if (!currentGroup) return;

    try {
      const result = await api(
        '/api/groups/' + encodeURIComponent(currentGroup.id) + '/join',
        {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({})}
      );

      notify(result.message || 'درخواست عضویت ثبت شد.');

      if (result.isMember) {
        await openGroup(currentGroup.id);
      }

      await loadGroups();

    } catch (error) {
      notify(error.message);
    }
  }

  async function leaveCurrentGroup() {
    if (!currentGroup) return;

    if (!confirm('از این گروه خارج می‌شوی؟')) return;

    try {
      const id = currentGroup.id;

      const result = await api(
        '/api/groups/' + encodeURIComponent(id) + '/leave',
        {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({})}
      );

      clearInterval(refreshTimer);
      currentGroup = null;

      notify(result.message || 'از گروه خارج شدی.');

      await backGroups();

    } catch (error) {
      notify(error.message);
    }
  }

  async function loadMessages(silent = false) {
    if (!currentGroup || loadingMessages) return;

    loadingMessages = true;

    try {
      const data = await api(
        '/api/groups/' + encodeURIComponent(currentGroup.id) + '/messages'
      );

      const next = Array.isArray(data.messages) ? data.messages : [];
      const sig = next.map(m => m.id + (m.editedAt || '') + JSON.stringify(m.reactions || []) + m.body.length).join('|');
      if (silent && sig === window.KC_GROUP_SIG) return;
      window.KC_GROUP_SIG = sig;
      currentMessages = next;

      renderMessages(silent);

    } catch (error) {
      if (!silent) notify(error.message);
    } finally {
      loadingMessages = false;
    }
  }

  function parseDate(value) {
    if (!value) return null;

    const normalized = String(value).replace(' ', 'T');

    const date = new Date(
      /Z$|[+-]\\d\\d:\\d\\d$/.test(normalized)
        ? normalized
        : normalized + 'Z'
    );

    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
  }

  function dateLabel(date) {
    const now = new Date();

    const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth(),now.getDate());
    const target = new Date(date.getFullYear(),date.getMonth(),date.getDate());

    const difference = Math.round((today-target)/86400000);

    if (difference === 0) return 'امروز';
    if (difference === 1) return 'دیروز';

    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR', {
      year:'numeric',
      month:'long',
      day:'numeric'
    }).format(date);
  }

  function renderMessages(silent) {
    const box = $('kcMessages');
    if (!box) return;

    const oldScroll = box.scrollTop;
    const wasNearBottom =
      box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight < 100;

    const previousCount = box.children.length;

    box.replaceChildren();

    if (!currentMessages.length) {
      const empty = document.createElement('div');
      empty.className = 'kc-social-empty';
      empty.textContent = 'هنوز پیامی در این گروه ارسال نشده است.';
      box.appendChild(empty);
      return;
    }

    let previousDate = '';
    let lastSender = null;
    let separated = false;

    currentMessages.forEach(message => {
      const date = parseDate(message.createdAt);

      if (date) {
        const key = date.toLocaleDateString();

        if (key !== previousDate) {
          const separator = document.createElement('div');
          separator.className = 'kc-date-separator';
          separator.textContent = dateLabel(date);
          box.appendChild(separator);
          previousDate = key;
          separated = true;
        }
      }

      const item = document.createElement('article');
      const mine = !!(window.KC_SOCIAL_USER_ID && message.senderId === window.KC_SOCIAL_USER_ID);
      const grouped = lastSender === message.senderId && !separated;
      lastSender = message.senderId;
      separated = false;

      item.className = 'kc-message' + (mine ? ' mine' : '') + (grouped ? ' grouped' : '');
      item.dataset.id = message.id;
      item.dataset.name = message.displayName || message.username || '';
      item.dataset.text = message.body;

      const author = document.createElement('button');
      author.type = 'button';
      author.className = 'kc-message-author';

      const avatar = document.createElement('span');
      avatar.className = 'kc-message-avatar';
      if (typeof avatarMarkup === 'function' && message.avatar !== undefined) {
        avatar.innerHTML = avatarMarkup(message.avatar, message.avatarImage);
      } else {
        avatar.textContent = String(message.displayName || 'ک').trim().charAt(0) || 'ک';
      }

      const authorName = document.createElement('span');
      authorName.className = 'kc-message-author-name';
      authorName.textContent = message.displayName || message.username || 'بازیکن';

      author.append(avatar, authorName);
      author.addEventListener('click', e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        window.KOREAN_CORE?.openProfile(message.senderId);
      });

      const body = document.createElement('div');
      body.className = 'kc-message-body';
      window.KC_MEDIA.renderBody(body, message.body);

      if (message.replyTo && message.replyBody) {
        const quote = document.createElement('div');
        quote.className = 'kc-reply-quote';
        const qn = document.createElement('b');
        qn.textContent = message.replyName || 'پیام';
        const qt = document.createElement('span');
        qt.textContent = window.KC_MEDIA.preview(message.replyBody).slice(0, 90);
        quote.append(qn, qt);
        quote.addEventListener('click', e => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          const target = box.querySelector('[data-id="' + message.replyTo + '"]');
          if (target) { target.scrollIntoView({block:'center', behavior:'smooth'}); target.classList.add('flash'); setTimeout(() => target.classList.remove('flash'), 1200); }
        });
        body.prepend(quote);
      }

      const time = document.createElement('div');
      time.className = 'kc-message-time';

      time.textContent = date
        ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR', {
            hour:'2-digit',
            minute:'2-digit',
            hour12:false
          }).format(date)
        : '';

      if (message.editedAt) time.textContent = 'ویرایش‌شده · ' + time.textContent;
      const reacts = window.KC_MEDIA.reactionsRow(message);
      item.append(author, body);
      if (reacts) item.append(reacts);
      item.append(time);
      box.appendChild(item);
    });

    if (!silent || wasNearBottom || box.children.length > previousCount) {
      box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
    } else {
      box.scrollTop = oldScroll;
    }
  }

  async function sendMessage(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!currentGroup) return;

    const input = $('kcMessageInput');
    const text = input.value.trim();

    if (!text) return;

    const button = $('kcSendForm').querySelector('button');
    button.disabled = true;

    try {
      await api(
        '/api/groups/' + encodeURIComponent(currentGroup.id) + '/messages',
        {
          method:'POST',
          body:JSON.stringify({text, replyTo: window.KC_REPLY?.id || undefined})
        }
      );

      input.value = '';
      window.KC_clearReply?.();
      await loadMessages();

    } catch (error) {
      notify(error.message);
    } finally {
      button.disabled = false;
      input.focus();
    }
  }

  async function backGroups() {
    clearInterval(refreshTimer);
    refreshTimer = null;
    currentGroup = null;

    $('kcGroupView').classList.add('kc-social-hidden');
    $('kcSocialHome').classList.remove('kc-social-hidden');

    await loadGroups();
    startGroupsRefresh();
  }

  async function initialize() {
    createUI();

    try {
      const result = await api('/api/me');

      if (result.user) {
        window.KC_SOCIAL_USER_ID = result.user.id;
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  document.addEventListener('kc:user', e => { window.KC_SOCIAL_USER_ID = e.detail?.id; });
  document.addEventListener('kc:logout', () => { window.KC_SOCIAL_USER_ID = null; closeSocial(); });

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initialize);

  async function sendRaw(text) {
    if (!currentGroup) return;
    await api('/api/groups/' + encodeURIComponent(currentGroup.id) + '/messages', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({text, replyTo: window.KC_REPLY?.id || undefined})
    });
    window.KC_clearReply?.();
    window.KC_GROUP_SIG = '';
    await loadMessages();
  }

  document.addEventListener('kc:reload', () => {
    if (currentGroup) { window.KC_GROUP_SIG = ''; loadMessages(true); }
  });

  window.KOREAN_SOCIAL = {
    sendRaw,
    currentId: () => currentGroup?.id || null,
    open: openSocial,
    close: closeSocial,
    refresh: loadGroups
  };
})();
` };

ASSETS["/core.js"] = { type: "application/javascript; charset=utf-8", etag: '03676544b5ae', text: `(() => {
  'use strict';

  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  let me = null;
  let currentChat = null;
  let privateRefreshTimer = null;
  let privateListRefreshTimer = null;
  let privateListLoading = false;
  let privateLoading = false;
  let privateMessageSignature = "";
  let currentProfile = null;
  let activeCategory = 'coin';

  const roles = {
    owner: '👑 Owner',
    admin: '🛡️ Admin',
    support: '🎧 Support',
    premium: '💎 Premium',
    player: '🎮 Player'
  };

  async function api(path, options = {}) {
    const r = await fetch(path, {
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      ...options,
      headers: {
        ...(options.body ? {'Content-Type':'application/json'} : {}),
        ...(options.headers || {})
      }
    });

    const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'خطای ارتباط با سرور');
    return data;
  }

  function notify(text) {
    const toast = $('toast');
    if (toast) {
      toast.textContent = text;
      toast.classList.add('show');
      setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 2600);
    } else alert(text);
  }

  function node(tag, cls, text) {
    const el = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) el.className = cls;
    if (text !== undefined) el.textContent = text;
    return el;
  }

  function avatarNode(cls, avatar, fallback = '✦', image = null) {
    const el = node('span', cls);
    if (typeof avatarMarkup === 'function' && avatar !== undefined && avatar !== null) {
      el.innerHTML = avatarMarkup(avatar, image);
      el.classList.add('has-avatar');
    } else el.textContent = fallback;
    return el;
  }

  function shell() {
    const profile = $('profileView');
    if (!profile || $('kcCorePages')) return;

    const top = profile.querySelector('.dash-top');
    if (!top) return;

    const pages = node('section', 'kc-core-pages');
    pages.id = 'kcCorePages';
    pages.hidden = true;
    pages.innerHTML = \`
      <section class="kc-core-page" id="kcPagePrivate">
        <div class="kc-core-heading">
          <div><small>KOREAN CORE</small><h2>پیام‌های خصوصی</h2></div>
          <span>✉</span>
        </div>
        <div class="kc-search-box">
          <input id="kcUserSearch" type="search" autocomplete="off"
            placeholder="جستجوی نام یا نام کاربری...">
          <div id="kcUserSearchResults" class="kc-search-results"></div>
        </div>
        <div id="kcPrivateList" class="kc-private-list"></div>
        <div id="kcPrivateChat" hidden>
          <button class="kc-core-back" id="kcPrivateBack">← گفتگوها</button>
          <h3 id="kcPrivateName"></h3>
          <div id="kcPrivateMessages" class="kc-private-messages"></div>
          <form id="kcPrivateForm" class="kc-private-form">
            <input id="kcPrivateInput" maxlength="4000" placeholder="پیامت را بنویس..." required>
            <button>➤</button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="kc-core-page" id="kcPageStore" hidden>
        <div class="kc-core-heading">
          <div><small>KOREAN CORE</small><h2>فروشگاه</h2></div>
          <span>✦</span>
        </div>
        <div id="kcStoreCategories" class="kc-store-categories"></div>
        <div id="kcStoreItems" class="kc-store-items"></div>
      </section>

      <section class="kc-core-page" id="kcPageGame" hidden>
        <div class="kc-core-heading">
          <div><small>KOREAN CORE</small><h2>رتبه‌بندی بازیکنان</h2></div>
          <span>🏆</span>
        </div>
        <div id="kcLbTabs" class="kc-store-categories"></div>
        <div id="kcLbList" class="kc-private-list"></div>
      </section>

      <section class="kc-core-page" id="kcPagePublic" hidden>
        <button class="kc-core-back" id="kcPublicBack">← بازگشت</button>
        <div id="kcPublicContent"></div>
      </section>
    \`;

    top.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', pages);

    const nav = node('nav', 'kc-core-nav');
    nav.id = 'kcCoreNav';

    const tabs = [
      ['profile', '♙', 'پروفایل'],
      ['groups', '♧', 'گروه‌ها'],
      ['private', '✉', 'پیوی'],
      ['store', '✦', 'فروشگاه'],
      ['game', '🏆', 'رتبه‌ها']
    ];

    tabs.forEach(([id, icon, label]) => {
      const button = node('button', 'kc-core-tab');
      button.type = 'button';
      button.dataset.tab = id;
      button.append(node('span', '', icon), node('small', '', label));
      button.addEventListener('click', () => selectTab(id));
      nav.appendChild(button);
    });

    profile.appendChild(nav);

    $('kcPrivateBack').addEventListener('click', () => showPrivateList(false));
    $('kcPublicBack').addEventListener('click', () => selectTab('profile'));
    $('kcPrivateForm').addEventListener('submit', sendPrivateMessage);
  }

  function selectTab(tab) {
    shell();
    if (tab !== "private") { clearPrivateRefresh(); clearPrivateListRefresh(); }

    const profile = $('profileView');
    const pages = $('kcCorePages');

    document.querySelectorAll('.kc-core-tab').forEach(b => {
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.tab === tab);
    });

    if (tab === 'profile') {
      pages.hidden = true;
      profile.classList.remove('kc-core-page-open');
      window.KOREAN_SOCIAL?.close();
      return;
    }

    if (tab === 'groups') {
      pages.hidden = true;
      profile.classList.remove('kc-core-page-open');
      window.KOREAN_SOCIAL?.open();
      return;
    }

    window.KOREAN_SOCIAL?.close();
    profile.classList.add('kc-core-page-open');
    pages.hidden = false;

    document.querySelectorAll('.kc-core-page').forEach(p => p.hidden = true);

    if (tab === 'private') {
      $('kcPagePrivate').hidden = false;
      showPrivateList();
    }

    if (tab === 'store') {
      $('kcPageStore').hidden = false;
      loadStore();
    }

    if (tab === 'game') {
      $('kcPageGame').hidden = false;
      loadLeaderboard();
    }
  }

  function roleBadge(role) {
    return roles[role] || roles.player;
  }

  async function openProfile(id) {
    if (!id) return;

    try {
      const data = await api('/api/core/profile/' + encodeURIComponent(id));
      currentProfile = data.profile;
      selectTab('profile');

      const profile = $('profileView');
      profile.classList.add('kc-core-page-open');
      $('kcCorePages').hidden = false;
      document.querySelectorAll('.kc-core-page').forEach(p => p.hidden = true);
      $('kcPagePublic').hidden = false;

      const box = $('kcPublicContent');
      box.replaceChildren();

      const card = node('div', 'kc-public-card');
      const avatar = avatarNode('kc-public-avatar', currentProfile.avatar, '✦', currentProfile.avatarImage);
      const name = node('h2', '', currentProfile.displayName || currentProfile.username);
      const username = node('div', 'kc-public-username', '@' + currentProfile.username);
      const badge = node('div', 'kc-public-role', roleBadge(currentProfile.role));
      const bio = node('p', 'kc-public-bio', currentProfile.bio || 'بدون بیوگرافی');

      if (currentProfile.bannerImage && /^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i.test(currentProfile.bannerImage)) {
        const ban = node('div', 'kc-public-banner');
        ban.style.backgroundImage = \`url("/api/img/\${currentProfile.bannerImage}")\`;
        card.appendChild(ban);
        card.classList.add('has-banner');
      }

      card.append(avatar, name, username, badge, bio);

      const stats = node('div', 'kc-public-stats');
      [
        ['سطح', currentProfile.level],
        ['پیام‌ها', currentProfile.messages],
        ['کوین', currentProfile.balance],
        ['استارز', currentProfile.stars]
      ].forEach(([label, value]) => {
        const item = node('div', 'kc-public-stat');
        const formatted = value === '∞' ? '∞' : Number(value || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR');
        item.append(node('strong', '', formatted), node('small', '', label));
        stats.appendChild(item);
      });

      card.appendChild(stats);

      if (me && currentProfile.id !== me.id) {
        const actions = node('div', 'kc-public-actions');

        const dm = node('button', '', '✉ پیام خصوصی');
        const gift = node('button', '', '🎁 ارسال هدیه');
        const block = node('button', '', '🚫 مسدود کردن');
        const report = node('button', '', '⚑ گزارش کاربر');

        gift.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          let senderProfile;
          try {
            const own = await api('/api/core/profile/' + encodeURIComponent(me.id));
            senderProfile = own.profile;
          } catch (e) {
            notify('دریافت موجودی شما ناموفق بود: ' + e.message);
            return;
          }

          const overlay = node('div', 'kc-gift-modal-overlay');
          const modal = node('div', 'kc-gift-modal');
          const title = node('h3', '', '🎁 ارسال هدیه');
          const description = node('p', 'kc-gift-modal-description',
            'هدیه‌ای برای ' + (currentProfile.displayName || currentProfile.username));
          const currency = document.createElement('select');
          currency.className = 'kc-gift-modal-control';
          [
            ['coins', '🪙 کوین'],
            ['stars', '⭐ استارز']
          ].forEach(([value, label]) => {
            const option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = value;
            option.textContent = label;
            currency.appendChild(option);
          });

          const balance = node('div', 'kc-gift-modal-balance');
          const amount = document.createElement('input');
          amount.className = 'kc-gift-modal-control';
          amount.type = 'number';
          amount.min = '1';
          amount.step = '1';
          amount.inputMode = 'numeric';
          amount.placeholder = 'مقدار هدیه';

          const actionsRow = node('div', 'kc-gift-modal-actions');
          const cancel = node('button', 'kc-gift-modal-cancel', 'انصراف');
          const submit = node('button', 'kc-gift-modal-submit', 'ارسال هدیه');

          const formatBalance = value =>
            value === '∞' ? '∞' : Number(value || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR');

          const updateBalance = () => {
            const isCoins = currency.value === 'coins';
            const value = isCoins ? senderProfile.balance : senderProfile.stars;
            balance.textContent = 'موجودی شما: ' + formatBalance(value) +
              (isCoins ? ' کوین' : ' استارز');
          };

          currency.addEventListener('change', updateBalance);
          cancel.addEventListener('click', () => overlay.remove());
          overlay.addEventListener('click', event => {
            if (event.target === overlay) overlay.remove();
          });

          submit.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const value = Number(amount.value);
            if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value <= 0) {
              notify('مقدار هدیه باید یک عدد صحیح و بیشتر از صفر باشد.');
              return;
            }

            const available = currency.value === 'coins'
              ? senderProfile.balance : senderProfile.stars;
            if (available !== '∞' && value > Number(available || 0)) {
              notify('موجودی شما برای این هدیه کافی نیست.');
              return;
            }

            submit.disabled = true;
            submit.textContent = 'در حال ارسال...';

            try {
              const chatResult = await api('/api/core/private', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: JSON.stringify({targetUserId: currentProfile.id})
              });

              await api('/api/core/private/' + chatResult.chat.id + '/gift', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: JSON.stringify({currency: currency.value, amount: value})
              });

              overlay.remove();
              currentChat = chatResult.chat.id;
              selectTab('private');
              await openPrivateChat(currentChat,
                currentProfile.displayName || currentProfile.username);
              notify('هدیه با موفقیت ارسال شد.');
            } catch (e) {
              submit.disabled = false;
              submit.textContent = 'ارسال هدیه';
              notify(e.message);
            }
          });

          actionsRow.append(cancel, submit);
          modal.append(title, description, currency, balance, amount, actionsRow);
          overlay.appendChild(modal);
          document.body.appendChild(overlay);
          updateBalance();
          amount.focus();
        });

        dm.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          try {
            const result = await api('/api/core/private', {
              method: 'POST',
              body: JSON.stringify({targetUserId: currentProfile.id})
            });
            selectTab("private");
            await openPrivateChat(result.chat?.id || result.chat);
          } catch (e) { notify(e.message); }
        });

        block.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          if (!confirm('این کاربر مسدود شود؟')) return;
          try {
            const result = await api('/api/core/blocks', {
              method: 'POST',
              body: JSON.stringify({targetUserId: currentProfile.id})
            });
            notify(result.message);
          } catch (e) { notify(e.message); }
        });

        report.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const overlay = node('div', 'kc-gift-modal-overlay');
          const modal = node('div', 'kc-gift-modal');
          const title = node('h3', '', '⚑ گزارش بازیکن');
          const description = node('p', 'kc-gift-modal-description',
            'دلیل مناسب را انتخاب کن.');
          const select = document.createElement('select');
          select.className = 'kc-gift-modal-control';

          [
            ['spam', 'ارسال اسپم و تبلیغات'],
            ['insult', 'توهین و بی‌احترامی'],
            ['harassment', 'مزاحمت و آزار'],
            ['scam', 'کلاهبرداری'],
            ['impersonation', 'جعل هویت'],
            ['inappropriate', 'محتوای نامناسب'],
            ['privacy', 'نقض حریم خصوصی'],
            ['threat', 'تهدید'],
            ['child_safety', 'نگرانی درباره امنیت کودک'],
            ['other', 'سایر موارد']
          ].forEach(([value, label]) => {
            const option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = value;
            option.textContent = label;
            select.appendChild(option);
          });

          const details = document.createElement('textarea');
          details.className = 'kc-gift-modal-control';
          details.rows = 3;
          details.maxLength = 1000;
          details.placeholder = 'توضیحات بیشتر (اختیاری)';

          const row = node('div', 'kc-gift-modal-actions');
          const cancel = node('button', 'kc-gift-modal-cancel', 'انصراف');
          const send = node('button', 'kc-gift-modal-submit', 'ثبت گزارش');

          cancel.addEventListener('click', () => overlay.remove());
          overlay.addEventListener('click', event => {
            if (event.target === overlay) overlay.remove();
          });

          send.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            send.disabled = true;
            send.textContent = 'در حال ثبت...';
            try {
              const result = await api('/api/core/reports', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: JSON.stringify({
                  targetUserId: currentProfile.id,
                  reason: select.value,
                  details: details.value.trim()
                })
              });
              overlay.remove();
              notify(result.message);
            } catch (e) {
              send.disabled = false;
              send.textContent = 'ثبت گزارش';
              notify(e.message);
            }
          });

          row.append(cancel, send);
          modal.append(title, description, select, details, row);
          overlay.appendChild(modal);
          document.body.appendChild(overlay);
        });

        actions.append(dm, gift, block, report);
        card.appendChild(actions);
      }

      box.appendChild(card);
    } catch (e) {
      notify(e.message);
    }
  }

  function clearPrivateRefresh() {
    clearInterval(privateRefreshTimer);
    privateRefreshTimer = null;
  }

  function clearPrivateListRefresh() {
    clearInterval(privateListRefreshTimer);
    privateListRefreshTimer = null;
  }

  async function showPrivateList(silent = false) {
    if (privateListLoading) return;

    clearPrivateRefresh();
    currentChat = null;
    privateMessageSignature = '';

    $('kcPrivateChat').hidden = true;
    $('kcPrivateList').hidden = false;

    if (!silent) {
      $('kcPrivateList').replaceChildren(
        node('div', 'kc-core-empty', 'در حال دریافت گفتگوها...')
      );
    }

    privateListLoading = true;

    try {
      const data = await api('/api/core/private');

      if (
        currentChat !== null ||
        $('kcPrivateList').hidden ||
        $('kcPagePrivate').hidden
      ) return;

      const list = $('kcPrivateList');
      list.replaceChildren();

      if (!data.chats.length) {
        list.appendChild(node(
          'div',
          'kc-core-empty',
          'هنوز گفتگوی خصوصی نداری. برای شروع، روی آواتار یک کاربر بزن.'
        ));
        return;
      }

      data.chats.forEach(chat => {
        const item = node('button', 'kc-private-item');
        item.type = 'button';
        item.append(
          avatarNode('kc-private-avatar', chat.avatar, '✦', chat.avatarImage),
          node('span', 'kc-private-info')
        );

        const info = item.querySelector('.kc-private-info');
        info.append(
          node('strong', '', chat.displayName || chat.username),
          node('small', '', chat.lastMessage ? window.KC_MEDIA.preview(chat.lastMessage) : roleBadge(chat.role))
        );

        const unread = Number(chat.unreadCount || 0);
        if (unread > 0) {
          item.appendChild(node(
            'span',
            'kc-unread-badge',
            unread > 99 ? '۹۹+' : unread.toLocaleString('fa-IR')
          ));
        }

        item.addEventListener('click', () =>
          openPrivateChat(chat.chatId, chat.displayName)
        );

        list.appendChild(item);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!silent) notify(e.message);
    } finally {
      privateListLoading = false;
    }

    if (!privateListRefreshTimer) {
      privateListRefreshTimer = setInterval(() => {
        if (
          document.visibilityState === 'visible' &&
          currentChat === null &&
          !$('kcPrivateList').hidden &&
          !$('kcPagePrivate').hidden
        ) {
          showPrivateList(true);
        }
      }, 3000);
    }
  }

  async function openPrivateChat(chatId, title = 'گفتگوی خصوصی') {
    const id = typeof chatId === 'object' ? chatId?.id : chatId;
    if (!id) {
      notify('شناسه گفتگوی خصوصی معتبر نیست.');
      return;
    }

    clearPrivateRefresh();
    clearPrivateListRefresh();
    currentChat = String(id);
    privateMessageSignature = '';

    $('kcPrivateList').hidden = true;
    $('kcPrivateChat').hidden = false;
    $('kcPrivateName').textContent = title;

    await loadPrivateMessages();

    if (currentChat) {
      const activeChat = currentChat;

      privateRefreshTimer = setInterval(() => {
        if (
          document.visibilityState === 'visible' &&
          currentChat === activeChat &&
          !$('kcPrivateChat').hidden
        ) {
          loadPrivateMessages(true);
        }
      }, 1500);
    }
  }

  async function loadPrivateMessages(silent = false) {
    if (!currentChat || privateLoading) return;
    if (document.visibilityState !== 'visible') return;

    const chatId = currentChat;
    privateLoading = true;

    try {
      const data = await api(
        '/api/core/private/' + encodeURIComponent(chatId) + '/messages'
      );

      if (currentChat !== chatId) return;

      const messages = Array.isArray(data.messages) ? data.messages : [];
      const signature = JSON.stringify(
        messages.map(m => [m.id, m.body, m.createdAt, m.editedAt, m.reactions])
      );

      if (signature === privateMessageSignature) return;

      const box = $('kcPrivateMessages');
      if (!box) return;

      const wasNearBottom =
        box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight < 100;

      box.replaceChildren();

      messages.forEach(message => {
        let giftEvent = null;

        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(message.body);
          if (parsed && parsed.type === 'core_gift') {
            giftEvent = parsed;
          }
        } catch (_) {}

        if (giftEvent) {
          const card = node(
            'div',
            'kc-gift-event' +
              (message.senderId === me?.id ? ' mine' : '')
          );

          card.append(
            node('div', 'kc-gift-icon', '🎁'),
            node('strong', 'kc-gift-title', 'هدیه جدید'),
            node(
              'p',
              'kc-gift-amount',
              \`\${Number(giftEvent.amount).toLocaleString('fa-IR')} \${giftEvent.currency === 'coins' ? 'کوین' : 'استارز'}\`
            ),
            node(
              'small',
              'kc-gift-names',
              \`\${giftEvent.senderName || message.displayName} ← \${giftEvent.recipientName || 'بازیکن'}\`
            ),
            node(
              'small',
              'kc-gift-time',
              new Date(
                giftEvent.createdAt || message.createdAt
              ).toLocaleString('fa-IR')
            )
          );

          box.appendChild(card);
          return;
        }

        const item = node(
          'div',
          'kc-private-message' +
            (message.senderId === me?.id ? ' mine' : '')
        );

        const mineMsg = message.senderId === me?.id;
        if (!mineMsg) item.append(node('strong', '', message.displayName || message.username));
        const textEl = node('p', '');
        window.KC_MEDIA.renderBody(textEl, message.body);
        item.append(textEl);
        item.dataset.id = message.id;
        const reacts = window.KC_MEDIA.reactionsRow(message);
        if (reacts) item.append(reacts);
        const stamp = new Date(String(message.createdAt).replace(' ', 'T') + 'Z');
        if (!isNaN(stamp)) item.append(node('time', 'kc-private-time',
          (message.editedAt ? 'ویرایش‌شده · ' : '') +
          new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR', {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', hour12:false}).format(stamp)));
        item.dataset.text = message.body;

        box.appendChild(item);
      });

      privateMessageSignature = signature;

      if (wasNearBottom || !privateMessageSignature.length || true) {
        if (wasNearBottom || window.KC_FIRST_PRIVATE !== chatId) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
        window.KC_FIRST_PRIVATE = chatId;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (!silent) notify(e.message);
    } finally {
      privateLoading = false;
    }
  }

  async function sendPrivateMessage(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!currentChat) return;

    const input = $('kcPrivateInput');
    const text = input.value.trim();
    if (!text) return;

    try {
      await api('/api/core/private/' + currentChat + '/messages', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({text})
      });
      input.value = '';
      await loadPrivateMessages();
    } catch (e) {
      notify(e.message);
    }
  }

  const categories = [
    ['coin', 'کوین'],
    ['stars', 'استارز'],
    ['message_frame', 'کادر پیام'],
    ['avatar', 'آواتار'],
    ['player_frame', 'کادر بازیکن']
  ];

  async function loadStore() {
    const cats = $('kcStoreCategories');
    cats.replaceChildren();

    categories.forEach(([id, label]) => {
      const button = node('button', 'kc-store-category', label);
      button.classList.toggle('active', activeCategory === id);
      button.addEventListener('click', () => {
        activeCategory = id;
        loadStore();
      });
      cats.appendChild(button);
    });

    const box = $('kcStoreItems');
    box.replaceChildren(node('div', 'kc-core-empty', 'در حال دریافت محصولات...'));

    try {
      const data = await api('/api/core/shop');
      box.replaceChildren();

      const items = data.items.filter(x => x.category === activeCategory);

      if (!items.length) {
        box.appendChild(node('div', 'kc-core-empty', 'محصولی در این دسته ثبت نشده است.'));
        return;
      }

      items.forEach(item => {
        const card = node('article', 'kc-store-item');
        card.append(
          node('div', 'kc-store-icon', '✦'),
          node('h3', '', item.name),
          node('p', '', item.description || ''),
          node('strong', '', Number(item.priceAmount).toLocaleString('fa-IR') +
            (item.priceCurrency === 'stars' ? ' ⭐' :
             item.priceCurrency === 'coins' ? ' 🪙' : ''))
        );

        const buy = node('button', 'kc-store-buy',
          item.priceCurrency === 'real' ? 'به‌زودی' : 'خرید');
        buy.disabled = item.priceCurrency === 'real';

        buy.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          try {
            const result = await api('/api/core/shop/buy', {
              method: 'POST',
              body: JSON.stringify({itemId: item.id})
            });
            notify(result.message);
            window.KC_refreshUser?.();
          } catch (e) { notify(e.message); }
        });

        card.appendChild(buy);
        box.appendChild(card);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      box.replaceChildren(node('div', 'kc-core-empty', e.message));
    }
  }


  let lbBy = 'coins';

  async function loadLeaderboard() {
    const tabs = $('kcLbTabs'), list = $('kcLbList');
    tabs.replaceChildren();
    [['coins', '🪙 ثروت'], ['level', '⚡ سطح'], ['messages', '💬 فعالیت']].forEach(([id, label]) => {
      const b = node('button', 'kc-store-category', label);
      b.classList.toggle('active', lbBy === id);
      b.addEventListener('click', () => { lbBy = id; loadLeaderboard(); });
      tabs.appendChild(b);
    });

    list.replaceChildren(node('div', 'kc-core-empty', 'در حال دریافت رتبه‌ها...'));

    try {
      const data = await api('/api/core/leaderboard?by=' + lbBy);
      list.replaceChildren();
      if (!data.users.length) { list.appendChild(node('div', 'kc-core-empty', 'هنوز بازیکنی ثبت نشده است.')); return; }

      data.users.forEach((u, i) => {
        const item = node('button', 'kc-private-item kc-lb-item' + (u.id === data.me ? ' me' : ''));
        item.type = 'button';
        const rank = node('span', 'kc-lb-rank', ['🥇', '🥈', '🥉'][i] || (i + 1).toLocaleString('fa-IR'));
        const info = node('span', 'kc-private-info');
        const value = lbBy === 'coins' ? Number(u.balance).toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' 🪙'
          : lbBy === 'level' ? 'سطح ' + Number(u.level).toLocaleString('fa-IR')
          : Number(u.messages).toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' پیام';
        info.append(node('strong', '', u.displayName || u.username), node('small', '', value));
        item.append(rank, avatarNode('kc-private-avatar', u.avatar, '✦', u.avatarImage), info);
        item.addEventListener('click', () => openProfile(u.id));
        list.appendChild(item);
      });
    } catch (e) { list.replaceChildren(node('div', 'kc-core-empty', e.message)); }
  }

  let userSearchTimer = null;
  let groupSearchTimer = null;

  async function searchUsers(query) {
    const box = $('kcUserSearchResults');
    if (!box) return;

    if (query.length < 2) {
      box.replaceChildren();
      return;
    }

    try {
      const data = await api('/api/core/search/users?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query));
      box.replaceChildren();

      if (!data.users.length) {
        box.appendChild(node('div', 'kc-search-empty', 'کاربری پیدا نشد.'));
        return;
      }

      data.users.forEach(user => {
        const item = node('button', 'kc-search-result');
        item.type = 'button';

        const avatar = avatarNode('kc-search-avatar', user.avatar, String(user.displayName || user.username || 'ک').charAt(0), user.avatarImage);

        const info = node('span', 'kc-search-info');
        info.append(
          node('strong', '', user.displayName || user.username),
          node('small', '', '@' + user.username)
        );

        item.append(avatar, info);
        item.addEventListener('click', () => openProfile(user.id));
        box.appendChild(item);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      box.replaceChildren(node('div', 'kc-search-empty', e.message));
    }
  }

  async function searchGroups(query) {
    const box = $('kcGroupSearchResults');
    if (!box) return;

    if (query.length < 2) {
      box.replaceChildren();
      return;
    }

    try {
      const data = await api('/api/core/search/groups?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query));
      box.replaceChildren();

      if (!data.groups.length) {
        box.appendChild(node('div', 'kc-search-empty', 'گروهی پیدا نشد.'));
        return;
      }

      data.groups.forEach(group => {
        const card = node('div', 'kc-group-search-result');

        const info = node('div', 'kc-search-info');
        info.append(
          node('strong', '', group.name),
          node('small', '', Number(group.memberCount || 0).toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' عضو')
        );

        if (group.description) {
          info.appendChild(node('p', '', group.description));
        }

        const action = node(
          'button',
          'kc-group-search-action',
          Number(group.isMember) === 1 ? 'عضو هستی' : 'درخواست عضویت'
        );

        action.disabled = Number(group.isMember) === 1;

        action.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          try {
            action.disabled = true;
            const result = await api('/api/groups/' + group.id + '/join', {
              method: 'POST',
              body: JSON.stringify({})
            });

            notify(result.message || 'درخواست ثبت شد.');

            if (result.pending) {
              action.textContent = 'در انتظار تأیید';
            } else {
              action.textContent = 'عضو شدی';
              window.KOREAN_SOCIAL?.open();
            }
          } catch (e) {
            action.disabled = false;
            notify(e.message);
          }
        });

        card.append(info, action);
        box.appendChild(card);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      box.replaceChildren(node('div', 'kc-search-empty', e.message));
    }
  }

  function injectGroupSearch() {
    const panel = $('kcSocialPanel');
    if (!panel || $('kcGroupSearch')) return;

    const section = node('section', 'kc-group-search');
    section.id = 'kcGroupSearch';

    const title = node('h3', '', 'جستجوی گروه‌ها');
    const input = node('input', '');
    input.id = 'kcGroupSearchInput';
    input.type = 'search';
    input.autocomplete = 'off';
    input.placeholder = 'نام گروه را وارد کن...';

    const results = node('div', 'kc-search-results');
    results.id = 'kcGroupSearchResults';

    section.append(title, input, results);
    panel.prepend(section);

    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
      clearTimeout(groupSearchTimer);
      const q = input.value.trim();
      groupSearchTimer = setTimeout(() => searchGroups(q), 300);
    });
  }

  function setupSearch() {
    const userInput = $('kcUserSearch');

    if (userInput && !userInput.dataset.ready) {
      userInput.dataset.ready = '1';
      userInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
        clearTimeout(userSearchTimer);
        const q = userInput.value.trim();
        userSearchTimer = setTimeout(() => searchUsers(q), 300);
      });
    }

    injectGroupSearch();
  }

  async function initialize() {
    shell();
    setupSearch();

    try {
      const data = await api('/api/me');
      me = data.user || null;
    } catch {}

    const profileRoot = $('profileView');
    if (profileRoot && !window.kcSearchObserver) {
      window.kcSearchObserver = new MutationObserver(() => {
        injectGroupSearch();
      });
      window.kcSearchObserver.observe(profileRoot, {
        childList: true,
        subtree: true
      });
    }

    document.addEventListener('kc:user', e => { me = e.detail; });
    document.addEventListener('kc:logout', () => { me = null; clearPrivateRefresh(); clearPrivateListRefresh(); });

    document.addEventListener('kc:reload', () => {
      if (currentChat) { privateMessageSignature = ''; loadPrivateMessages(true); }
    });

    window.KOREAN_CORE = {
      currentChatId: () => currentChat,
      sendPrivateRaw: async text => {
        if (!currentChat) return;
        await api('/api/core/private/' + currentChat + '/messages', {
          method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({text})
        });
        await loadPrivateMessages();
      },
      openProfile,
      selectTab,
      refresh: () => showPrivateList(false)
    };
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initialize);
  } else {
    initialize();
  }
})();
` };

  async function startRecording(kind) {
