    const form = kind === 'group' ? $('kcSendForm') : $('kcPrivateForm');
    const bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.className = 'kc-rec';
    const dot = document.createElement('span'); dot.className = 'kc-rec-dot';
    const clock = document.createElement('b'); clock.textContent = '0:00';
    const hint = document.createElement('span'); hint.className = 'kc-rec-hint'; hint.textContent = 'در حال ضبط...';
    const stop = send => { if (mr.state !== 'inactive') { rec.send = send; mr.stop(); } };
    bar.append(dot, clock, hint, mkBtn('✕', () => stop(false), 'kc-rec-x'), mkBtn('➤', () => stop(true), 'kc-rec-ok'));
    form.before(bar);

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const sec = Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000);
      clock.textContent = Math.floor(sec / 60) + ':' + String(sec % 60).padStart(2, '0');
      if (sec >= 120) stop(true);
    }, 500);

    rec = { send: false };
    mr.ondataavailable = e => { if (e.data.size) { chunks.push(e.data); size += e.data.size; if (size > 520000) stop(true); } };
    mr.onstop = async () => {
      clearInterval(timer); bar.remove(); stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
      const wantSend = rec?.send; rec = null;
      if (!wantSend) return;
      const dur = Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000);
      if (dur < 1) return say('پیام صوتی خیلی کوتاه بود.');
      try {
        say('در حال ارسال پیام صوتی...');
        const mime = (mr.mimeType || type || 'audio/webm').split(';')[0];
        const id = await M().uploadAudio(new Blob(chunks, { type: mime }), mime);
        const body = JSON.stringify({ type: 'kc_voice', id, dur });
        if (kind === 'group') await window.KOREAN_SOCIAL.sendRaw(body); else await window.KOREAN_CORE.sendPrivateRaw(body);
      } catch (e) { say(e.message); }
    };
    mr.start(1000);
    navigator.vibrate?.(15);
  }

  /* ---------- presence, typing indicator, pinned message ---------- */
  function chatContext() {
    const pc = $('kcPrivateChat');
    if (pc && !pc.hidden && window.KOREAN_CORE?.currentChatId?.()) return { scope: 'private', id: window.KOREAN_CORE.currentChatId() };
    const gv = $('kcGroupView');
    if (gv && !gv.classList.contains('kc-social-hidden') && window.KOREAN_SOCIAL?.currentId?.()) return { scope: 'group', id: window.KOREAN_SOCIAL.currentId() };
    return null;
  }
  const ago = sec => {
    if (sec == null) return 'آفلاین';
    if (sec < 120) return 'لحظاتی پیش';
    if (sec < 3600) return Math.round(sec / 60).toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' دقیقه پیش';
    if (sec < 86400) return Math.round(sec / 3600).toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' ساعت پیش';
    return Math.round(sec / 86400).toLocaleString('fa-IR') + ' روز پیش';
  };
  function lineFor(scope) {
    const box = $(scope === 'group' ? 'kcMessages' : 'kcPrivateMessages');
    if (!box) return {};
    let status = box.parentElement.querySelector('.kc-status-line');
    let pin = box.parentElement.querySelector('.kc-pin');
    if (!status) { status = document.createElement('div'); status.className = 'kc-status-line'; box.before(status); }
    if (!pin && scope === 'group') { pin = document.createElement('div'); pin.className = 'kc-pin'; pin.hidden = true; status.before(pin); }
    return { status, pin };
  }
  let polling = false;
  async function poll() {
    const ctx = chatContext();
    if (!ctx || polling || document.hidden) return;
    polling = true;
    try {
      const d = await api('GET', \`/api/core/typing?scope=\${ctx.scope}&id=\${encodeURIComponent(ctx.id)}\`);
      const { status, pin } = lineFor(ctx.scope);
      if (!status) return;
      if (d.typing?.length) {
        status.textContent = '✍️ ' + d.typing.slice(0, 2).join('، ') + ' در حال نوشتن...';
        status.className = 'kc-status-line typing';
      } else if (ctx.scope === 'private' && d.peer) {
        status.textContent = d.peer.online ? '🟢 آنلاین' : '⚪ آخرین بازدید: ' + ago(d.peer.agoSec);
        status.className = 'kc-status-line' + (d.peer.online ? ' online' : '');
      } else { status.textContent = ''; status.className = 'kc-status-line empty'; }

      if (pin) {
        if (d.pin) {
          pin.hidden = false; pin.replaceChildren();
          const t = document.createElement('span'); t.textContent = '📌 ' + d.pin.displayName + ': ' + M().preview(d.pin.body).slice(0, 70);
          t.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const el = document.querySelector(\`#kcMessages [data-id="\${d.pin.id}"]\`);
            if (el) { el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'smooth' }); el.classList.add('flash'); setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('flash'), 1200); }
          });
          pin.append(t, mkBtn('✕', async () => {
            try { await api('POST', '/api/core/pin', { groupId: ctx.id, action: 'unpin' }); pin.hidden = true; } catch (e) { say(e.message); }
          }));
        } else pin.hidden = true;
      }
    } catch {} finally { polling = false; }
  }
  setInterval(poll, 3000);

  let lastTyping = 0;
  document.addEventListener('input', e => {
    if (!e.target.closest?.('#kcSendForm,#kcPrivateForm') || !e.target.value) return;
    const now = Date.now(); if (now - lastTyping < 2500) return;
    const ctx = chatContext(); if (!ctx) return;
    lastTyping = now;
    api('POST', '/api/core/typing', ctx).catch(() => {});
  });

  const ping = () => { if (window.KC_USER && !document.hidden) api('POST', '/api/core/ping', {}).catch(() => {}); };
  setInterval(ping, 45000);
  document.addEventListener('kc:user', ping);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', ping);

  /* ---------- swipe to reply (group chat) ---------- */
  function swipeReply() {
    const box = $('kcMessages'); if (!box || box.dataset.swipe) return;
    box.dataset.swipe = '1';
    let sx = 0, sy = 0, el = null, fired = false;
    box.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
      const t = e.touches[0]; el = e.target.closest('.kc-message'); sx = t.clientX; sy = t.clientY; fired = false;
    }, { passive: true });
    box.addEventListener('touchmove', e => {
      if (!el) return;
      const t = e.touches[0], dx = t.clientX - sx, dy = t.clientY - sy;
      if (Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx) || Math.abs(dx) < 10) return;
      el.style.transform = \`translateX(\${Math.max(-70, Math.min(70, dx * 0.5))}px)\`;
      if (Math.abs(dx) > 90 && !fired) { fired = true; navigator.vibrate?.(12); }
    }, { passive: true });
    box.addEventListener('touchend', () => {
      if (!el) return;
      el.style.transform = '';
      if (fired && el.dataset.id) setReply({ id: el.dataset.id, name: el.dataset.name, text: el.dataset.text });
      el = null;
    });
  }

  /* ---------- keyboard-safe fullscreen (mobile) ---------- */
  if (window.visualViewport) {
    const fit = () => root.style.setProperty('--kc-vh', visualViewport.height + 'px');
    visualViewport.addEventListener('resize', () => { fit(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); });
    fit();
  }

  function setup() {
    addHeaderButtons();
    enhanceForm($('kcSendForm'), 'group'); enhanceForm($('kcPrivateForm'), 'private');
    addFab('kcMessages'); addFab('kcPrivateMessages'); swipeReply();
    addSearch('kcMessages', () => document.querySelector('#kcGroupView .kc-chat-header'));
    addSearch('kcPrivateMessages', () => $('kcPrivateChat'));
    if (query) applySearch();
  }

  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') ['.kc-sheet', '.kc-emoji-pop', '.kc-lightbox'].forEach(s => document.querySelector(s)?.remove());
  });

  applyAll();
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setup); else setup();
  setTimeout(setup, 400);
  let t = null;
  new MutationObserver(() => { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(setup, 60); }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
})();
` };

// ── /sw.js (1.1 KB)
ASSETS["/sw.js"] = { type: "application/javascript; charset=utf-8", etag: '3978301ad7ad', text: `const CACHE = 'kc-shell-v2';
const SHELL = ['/', '/style.css', '/social.css', '/core.css', '/glass.css', '/app.js', '/avatars.js', '/upload.js', '/social.js', '/core.js', '/chatplus.js', '/manifest.webmanifest'];

self.addEventListener('install', e => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.addAll(SHELL)).catch(() => {}));
  self.skipWaiting();
});
self.addEventListener('activate', e => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.keys().then(keys => Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE).map(k => caches.delete(k)))));
  self.clients.claim();
});
// Network-first for the app shell (so updates arrive instantly), cache as offline fallback. API is never cached.
self.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  const url = new URL(e.request.url);
  if (e.request.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== location.origin || url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) return;
  e.respondWith(
    fetch(e.request).then(r => {
      if (r.ok) { const copy = r.clone(); caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(e.request, copy)); }
      return r;
    }).catch(() => caches.match(e.request).then(r => r || caches.match('/')))
  );
});
` };

ASSETS['/index.html'] = ASSETS['/'];
ASSETS['/favicon.ico'] = ASSETS['/icon-192.png'];

const __bin = new Map();
const __bytes = a => {
  if (!__bin.has(a.etag)) __bin.set(a.etag, Uint8Array.from(atob(a.b64), c => c.charCodeAt(0)));
  return __bin.get(a.etag);
};

const SECURITY_HEADERS = {
  'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
  'Referrer-Policy': 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin',
  'X-Frame-Options': 'DENY',
  'Permissions-Policy': 'camera=(), geolocation=(), microphone=(self)'
};

function serveStatic(request, path) {
  const a = ASSETS[path];
  if (!a) return new Response('Not found', { status: 404, headers: SECURITY_HEADERS });
  const headers = {
    ...SECURITY_HEADERS,
    'Content-Type': a.type,
    'ETag': '"' + a.etag + '"',
    'Cache-Control': a.b64 ? 'public, max-age=86400' : 'no-cache'
  };
  if (request.headers.get('If-None-Match') === headers.ETag) return new Response(null, { status: 304, headers });
  const body = request.method === 'HEAD' ? null : (a.b64 ? __bytes(a) : a.text);
  return new Response(body, { headers });
}

<ol style="padding-right:20px;margin:0"><li>Settings ← Bindings ← Add ← <b>D1 database</b></li><li>اسم متغیر را دقیقاً <b>DB</b> بگذار و دیتابیس را انتخاب کن</li><li>Deploy را بزن و این صفحه را رفرش کن</li></ol></div></body></html>`;

export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const path = url.pathname;

    if (path.startsWith('/api/')) return __api.fetch(request, env, ctx);

    if (request.method !== 'GET' && request.method !== 'HEAD')
      return new Response('Method Not Allowed', { status: 405, headers: SECURITY_HEADERS });

    if (!env.DB && (path === '/' || path === '/index.html'))
      return new Response(SETUP_PAGE, { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', ...SECURITY_HEADERS } });

    return serveStatic(request, path);
  }
};
